Result: 指出架构进步在缺乏基准一致的评估下难以被合理解读；基于位移的开环度量只能提供有限的关于安全性与人类对齐的证据。基准正从开环轨迹匹配转向闭环仿真、非反应性真实日志评估、长尾场景测试和人类偏好感知指标。

Conclusion: 总结了若干尚未解决的关键挑战，包括不确定性感知规划、学习器-专家不匹配、运行时安全保障、语言-动作接地、世界模型验证以及可复现基准建设，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has evolved from camera-to-control regression toward planning-oriented systems that use structured representations, trajectory-level outputs, and increasingly realistic evaluation protocols. This survey reviews this transition across behavior cloning, conditional imitation learning, privileged distillation, BEV and vectorized planning, unified perception-prediction-planning architectures, world-model-based planners, and vision-language-action systems. We argue that the key distinction in modern end-to-end driving is not whether intermediate representations are used, but whether they are learned, supervised, and evaluated to support safe, feasible, and route-compliant planning. To organize the literature, we synthesize existing methods along four axes: input representation, planning output, supervision signal, and evaluation protocol. We further examine the benchmark shift from open-loop trajectory matching to closed-loop simulation, non-reactive real-log evaluation, long-tail testing, and human-preference-aware metrics. Our analysis highlights that architectural progress is difficult to interpret without benchmark-consistent evaluation, and that displacement-based open-loop metrics alone provide limited evidence for safe and human-aligned driving. We conclude with open challenges in uncertainty-aware planning, learner-expert mismatch, runtime safety assurance, language-action grounding, world-model validation, and reproducible benchmarking.

</details>


### [148] [Video2DoorTraversal: Push Door Traversal via Simulated Door Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20251)
*Xincheng Tang,Yiji Chen,Youhan Xie,Wanyu Li,Zhengjie Shu,Lai Jiang,Wenkang Hu,Yitong Li,Jinchuang Zhang,Xibin Song,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Video2DoorTraversal，一个基于单视频真-仿-真（real-to-sim-to-real）框架的轮腿移动机械臂开门与穿越任务系统，实现了96.57%的真实门平均成功率和80.95%的未见门零样本成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开门并穿越是一个长期的移动操控（loco-manipulation）任务，需要精确的把手交互和基座-手臂的协同控制，且现有方法难以从少量真实数据中高效迁移到仿真环境再部署回真实世界。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心组件：(1) DoorTwin，基于单个RGB视频重建实例对齐、可铰接、可直接仿真的门模型（含真实几何与外观）；(2) 仿真内嵌代理（simulation-in-the-loop agent），将恢复的铰接信息转换为参数化技能程序，并通过对失败滚动（rollout）的迭代细化来生成物理上可执行的示范；(3) ArticuACT，一种双深度（dual-depth）策略，利用机器人中心相机条件化和交互感知监督，预测基座、手臂和夹爪的协调指令。

Result: 系统全部感知与策略推断均板载运行，在五个真实门上达到96.57%的平均成功率，在结构相似的未见门上达到80.95%的零样本成功率，完整接近、开门和穿越序列平均约13秒完成。

Conclusion: 单视频驱动下的真实-仿真-真实闭环框架能够高效完成长时程的开门穿越任务，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Door opening and traversal is a long-horizon loco-manipulation task that requires precise handle interaction and coordinated base-arm control. We present Video2DoorTraversal, a single-video real-to-sim-to-real framework for wheel-legged mobile manipulators. Given one RGB video of a real door, DoorTwin reconstructs an instance-aligned, articulated, and simulation-ready door twin with realistic geometry and appearance. A simulation-in-the-loop agent converts the recovered articulation into a parameterized skill program and iteratively refines failed rollouts to generate physically executable demonstrations. These demonstrations are used to train ArticuACT, a dual-depth policy that predicts coordinated base, arm, and gripper commands using robot-centric camera conditioning and interaction-aware supervision. With all perception and policy inference running onboard, the system achieves a 96.57% average success rate across five real doors and an 80.95% zero-shot success rate on structurally similar unseen doors, while completing the full approach, opening, and traversal sequence in approximately 13s on average. Project Page: https://video2doortraversal.github.io/.

</details>


### [149] [DART-S: Reachability-Audited Active-Suspension Preconditioning for Off-Road Vehicle Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20275)
*Yu Hu,Fangzhou Zhao,Liang Chen,Chen Min,Wei Li,Mingyuan Sang,Jiajia Ma,Shican Chen,Di Pang,Baolei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-S是一种扩展的着陆恢复框架，通过在滑跑阶段对轮胎-地面接触施加斜坡面预置条件来改变起飞前的俯仰姿态、俯仰角速度和车轮转速，从而在轮式角动量预算之外获得额外的姿态纠正能力。在600次新运行和72个独立BeamNG会话中，所有查询均按预定分支执行，在40°/13 m/s边界上达到24/24的触地后姿态标准成功率（对照DART为0/24），且在11.5 m/s下通过0.35秒的时序动作实现23/24成功率（对照静态预设为0/24），同时200 rad/s指令保护使驱动系统硬限制零越界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DART-S旨在解决原DART方案的固有局限：空中轮式扭矩反作用无法在轮式角动量预算之外恢复起飞错误。为了突破这一权威预算限制，DART-S提出在起飞前通过斜坡面悬架预置来主动重塑系统状态，从而扩大可供姿态纠正使用的权威余量。

Method: DART-S采用三种关键机制：(1) 斜坡面悬架预置，在起飞前改变俯仰角、俯仰角速度和车轮自旋状态；(2) 局部校准映射，预测每个悬架动作如何重塑状态-预算对；(3) 支持感知选择器，将预测偏移与局部结果证据及区间可达性筛查结合，并通过精确配对审计报告残余权威。

Result: 在600次运行与72个独立BeamNG会话的验证中：所有正、负和边界查询均遵守预定分支；在40°/13 m/s边界上DART-S达到24/24次触地后姿态标准成功，而DART仅0/24（会话级Holm校正p=0.0234）；在11.5 m/s速度下，0.35秒时序动作实现23/24成功，静态预设为0/24（p=0.0156）；200 rad/s指令保护确保600次运行中零驱动系统硬限制越界。

Conclusion: DART-S通过将姿态纠正能力扩展到起飞前阶段，显著提升了超出轮式角动量预算的着陆恢复性能，并在严格的统计检验中获得了显著改进结果。源代码已开源。

Abstract: Airborne torque reaction cannot recover takeoff errors beyond the wheel angular-momentum budget. DART-S applies ramp-face suspension preconditioning to change pitch, pitch rate, and wheel spin before liftoff, thereby shifting the queried state and altering the remaining authority budget. To predict how each suspension action reshapes this state-budget pair, DART-S employs a local calibration map. A support-aware selector combines the predicted shift with local outcome evidence and an interval-reachability screen; an exact-pair audit reports residual authority. Across 600 new runs in 72 independent BeamNG sessions, every positive, negative, and boundary query follows its prespecified branch. At the confirmed 40°/13 m/s boundary, DART-S attains 24/24 post-touchdown attitude-criterion successes versus 0/24 for DART (session-level Holm-adjusted p=0.0234). At 11.5 m/s, a 0.35 s timing action attains 23/24 versus 0/24 for the static preset (p=0.0156). The 200 rad/s command guard keeps drivetrain hard-limit exceedance at zero across all 600 runs. The source code will be available at https://github.com/MeridianCAS/DART-S

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [HyperCut: Fast Inter-Layer Scheduling via Directed Hypergraph and Early Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19296)
*Ziang Wei,Zirui Xu,Sufeng Guo,Chuanchao Gao,Yiyang Gao,Arvind Easwaran,Yuxiang Fu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HyperCut 是一种基于超图划分的分层调度框架，通过在上层（层间）调度时利用有向超图抽象来预估层内调度的成本上界，从而实现早期剪枝，将设计空间从 O(9.899^N) 降至 O(N)，在 10 个评测案例中相比 SOTA 调度器 SET 实现 2.0x 性能提升和 80.47% 的探索时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层间调度器需要等待完整的细粒度层内调度完成后才能获得成本反馈，导致解耦流程反复探索次优甚至不可行的层间调度，且缺乏早期剪枝机制，成为 DNN 编译器设计空间探索的关键瓶颈。

Method: 核心观察：一旦层间切割确定了子网格形状，层内调度的成本即可被紧密上界界定，从而无需求解层内问题即可为每个层间候选方案评估成本。基于此，提出分层划分-映射框架 HyperCut：(1) 采用有向超图（DHG）抽象 DNN；(2) 引入统一表示 State，联合编码 DHG 划分、tile 网格分配和张量批量切分；(3) 将划分与映射耦合为统一优化目标，实现基于超图划分的层间调度早期过滤。

Result: 对于 N 层的 DNN，理论设计空间从 SOTA 开源调度器 SET 的 O(9.899^N) 降至 O(N)。在 10 个评测案例上，HyperCut 相比 SET 基线实现 2.0x 的几何平均性能提升，并减少 80.47% 的探索时间。

Conclusion: 通过在层间调度阶段引入基于超图划分的早期成本评估与剪枝，HyperCut 有效解决了层间与层内调度解耦带来的效率问题，大幅压缩了设计空间，同时显著提升性能与探索效率。

Abstract: As deep neural networks (DNNs) continue to scale, inter-layer scheduling, which orchestrates the spatial allocation of compute resources and the temporal execution order across layers, has become a decisive factor in sustaining high utilization and energy efficiency on tiled accelerators. However, existing inter-layer schedulers defer cost feedback until a complete fine-grained intra-layer scheduling has been resolved. The resulting decoupled flow repeatedly explores sub-optimal or even infeasible inter-layer schedules, and the absence of early pruning during the inter-layer phase remains a critical bottleneck for design-space exploration (DSE) in DNN compilers.
  Our key observation is that the cost of an intra-layer scheduling can be tightly upper-bounded once the inter-layer cut fixes the sub-mesh shape, which lets us cost every inter-layer candidate without solving the intra-layer problem. Hence, we propose a hierarchical partitioning-and-mapping framework, HyperCut, that enables early filtering of inter-layer schedules based on hypergraph partitioning. Based on the directed hypergraph (DHG) abstraction of DNN, we introduce a unified representation, State, that jointly encodes the DHG partition, tile mesh allocation and tensor batch splitting. Thereby, partitioning and mapping are coupled into a union optimization object. For a DNN with N layers, the resulting theoretical design space is bounded by O(N), compared with O(9.899^N) for the state-of-the-art open-source scheduler SET. Across 10 evaluated cases, HyperCut achieves 2.0x performance improvement and 80.47% exploration time reduction over the SET baseline, measured by geometric mean.

</details>


### [151] [Performance Verification of the AmpereOne CPU Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19300)
*Doa'a Al-Otoom,Nicholas Kelly,Aaron Lindsay,Agreen Ahmadi,Ananth Kumar,Anthony Faubert,Benjamin Chaffin,Bret Toll,Eric Wallace,Lucas Crowthers,Mark Charney,Michael Chin,Michael Spradling,Scott Witscher,Sriyash Caculo,Syed Fakhri,Vivek Kothapalli,William Freelove,Mahesh Madhav*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AmpereOne定制CPU核心四代产品中采用的工业级性能验证（PV）方法，以RTL设计与trace驱动性能模型之间的周期精确相关性为核心，结合数据驱动的工作负载筛选、高频回归系统和统一事件流分析框架，并通过分支预测单元与L2预取器的案例验证了先单元级后全核的分层验证策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工艺制程扩展放缓，微架构创新成为性能提升的主要驱动因素，使得硅前性能验证（PV）变得空前关键，需要一套系统化、可迭代的工业级验证方法来避免昂贵的硅后缺陷。

Method: 该方法论以RTL设计与trace驱动性能模型的周期精确相关性为核心，集成了三大支柱：数据驱动的工作负载筛选（确保覆盖真实场景）、高频每日回归系统（实现快速反馈）、以及统一事件流分析框架（便于对相关性进行深入分析）。验证采用分层策略，先对单个单元（如分支预测单元、L2预取器）进行隔离相关性验证，再进行全核级验证。

Result: 通过四代AmpereOne CPU核心的实际应用，该方法论成功避免了代价高昂的硅后性能缺陷，确保复杂处理器达到性能目标。案例研究表明分层验证策略在单元隔离与全核验证之间取得了有效平衡。

Conclusion: 这种有纪律、可迭代的性能验证过程对于确保现代复杂处理器满足性能目标至关重要，并展望了微处理器行业中性能验证的未来发展方向。

Abstract: As process technology scaling slows, microarchitectural innovation has become the primary driver of performance gains, making pre-silicon Performance Verification (PV) more critical than ever. This paper presents the industrial-scale PV methodology applied across four generations of the AmpereOne custom CPU core, centered on the cycle-accurate correlation of the RTL design against a trace-driven performance model. The methodology integrates data-driven workload curation, a high-frequency daily regression system, and a unified event-stream framework for analysis. We demonstrate this methodology through case studies of the Branch Prediction Unit and L2 Prefetcher, highlighting a hierarchical strategy that first isolates individual units for focused correlation before proceeding to full-core verification. The results demonstrate that this disciplined, iterative process is indispensable for avoiding costly post-silicon bugs and ensuring complex processors meet their performance targets. We end with a look towards the future of PV in the microprocessor industry.

</details>


### [152] [HYDRA: A Heterogeneous Chiplet DSE Framework for Serving Dynamic Hybrid LLM Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19395)
*Jiahao Lin,Alish Kanani,Sangwan Lee,Jaehyun Park,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HYDRA是一个面向混合Transformer-Mamba大语言模型在异构芯粒（chiplet）系统上服务的设计空间探索框架，通过联合探索芯片组成、布局、带宽配置、动态批处理和运行时调度，实现1.55倍吞吐量提升和43.7%的首令牌延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合Transformer-Mamba大语言模型虽能提升长上下文效率，但其异构的计算和通信模式使得硬件加速变得复杂。芯粒架构虽提供可扩展方案，但静态架构配置与动态运行时策略组成的设计空间过于庞大，难以穷举搜索，亟需一个系统化的设计空间探索框架。

Method: HYDRA框架联合探索芯粒组成、布局、芯粒间带宽配置、动态批处理和运行时调度。具体集成了通信感知布局（communication-aware placement）、动态批处理（dynamic batching）、弹性任务调度（elastic task scheduling），以及一个基于马尔可夫过程的快速性能估计器，用于捕捉多租户运行时动态，实现高效且准确的探索。

Result: 在所有工作负载下，HYDRA相比最先进的基线实现了平均1.55倍的吞吐量提升和43.7%的time-to-first-token（TTFT）降低，其中吞吐量增益最高可达2.3倍。

Conclusion: 架构与运行时策略的联合设计（co-design）对于在异构芯粒系统上实现高效的大规模LLM服务至关重要，HYDRA的实验结果充分验证了这一观点。

Abstract: Hybrid Transformer-Mamba large language models (LLMs) enhance long-context efficiency, but their heterogeneous computation and communication patterns complicate efficient hardware acceleration. Chiplet-based architectures offer a scalable solution by integrating specialized compute and memory units. However, the design space spanning static architectural configurations and dynamic runtime policies is prohibitively large to explore exhaustively. To address this challenge, we present HYDRA, a comprehensive design space exploration framework for hybrid LLM serving on heterogeneous chiplet systems. HYDRA jointly explores chiplet composition, placement, inter-chiplet bandwidth provisioning, dynamic batching, and runtime scheduling. It integrates communication-aware placement, dynamic batching, elastic task scheduling, and a fast Markov-based performance estimator that captures multi-tenant runtime dynamics for efficient and accurate exploration. Across all workloads, HYDRA delivers 1.55x the throughput and 43.7 percent lower time-to-first-token on average, with throughput gains reaching up to 2.3x compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results highlight that co-designing architecture and runtime policies is critical for efficient large-scale LLM serving on heterogeneous chiplet systems.

</details>


### [153] [Exact Multistate Reliability and Upgrade Design for Heterogeneous HBM Systems via Threshold-Pruned BAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19471)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确的多状态可靠性框架，用于高带宽内存（HBM）系统中部分服务的建模与分析。每个服务单元可携带任意有限带宽状态集合，可靠性定义为聚合带宽满足需求的概率。该框架将二元k-out-of-n模型作为特例，并提出阈值剪枝多状态二叉树（TP-mBAT）算法，在特定基准上相比动态规划大幅降低存储开销（从25.02 MB降至1,152 B）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HBM系统可能呈现部分服务（而非仅完全服务或完全隔离），即控制器可见的服务单元可因子通道隔离、通道重映射或保护开销等因素提供完整、降低或零带宽。现有二元k-out-of-n模型无法刻画这种多状态部分降级场景，需要精确的多状态可靠性建模框架。

Method: 提出精确的多状态可靠性框架，每个服务单元携带任意有限带宽状态集合；推导闭式二元映射误差关系用于均值带宽失真量化和简单抽象筛选；提出阈值剪枝的多状态二叉加法树（TP-mBAT）算法，包含三种精确剪枝规则（阈值剪枝、概率转移灵敏度剪枝、保留单元下限剪枝）；引入潜在包状态混合模型刻画共享应力依赖。

Result: 在紧凑公共网格上，16单元齐次控制只需273次剪枝DP更新（对比TP-mBAT的362,506次节点访问）；在14单元非齐次基准上，TP-mBAT与携带相同阈值规则的DP展开几乎相同的状态空间（6,862节点对6,861次更新），但保留状态差异显著：17个遍历条目对比412,121个概率状态，峰值存储从25.02 MB降至1,152 B；忽略依赖关系会高估可靠性8.73个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了HBM部分服务情形的精确多状态可靠性建模与高效求解方案，TP-mBAT在保留极小状态的同时保持精确性；动态规划在紧凑公共网格上更具优势，而TP-mBAT在非齐次场景下存储效率显著更优；包状态混合模型有效刻画了共享应力依赖对可靠性的实质影响。

Abstract: High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) systems can exhibit partial service rather than only full service or complete isolation: a controller-visible service unit may deliver full, reduced, or zero bandwidth because of sub-channel isolation, lane remapping, or protection overhead. This paper develops an exact multistate reliability framework in which each service unit carries an arbitrary finite set of bandwidth states and reliability is the probability that aggregate delivered bandwidth meets a demand. The binary k-out-of-n model is recovered as a special case, while closed-form binary-mapping error relations quantify mean-bandwidth distortion and provide a screening test for the simpler abstraction. For exact single-threshold evaluation a threshold-pruned multistate Binary-Addition-Tree (TP-mBAT) algorithm is proposed. It is deliberately regime-specific: fixed-grid dynamic programming is preferable on a compact common grid, where a 16-unit commensurate control required 273 pruned-DP updates versus 362,506 TP-mBAT node visits. On a reproducible 14-unit incommensurate benchmark, TP-mBAT is compared against a dynamic program carrying the same threshold rules, so that no baseline is weakened. Both expand the same state space, 6,862 nodes against 6,861 updates, and the separation lies entirely in retained state: 17 traversal entries against 412,121 probability states at central demand, reducing measured peak storage from 25.02 MB to 1,152 B. An exact probability-transfer sensitivity identifies when moving mass from a degraded state to a higher-bandwidth state changes system success, and a reserved-unit floor model admits a third exact pruning rule that is vacuous without such floors. A latent package-state mixture captures shared stress, where ignoring dependence overstates reliability by 8.73 percentage points.

</details>


### [154] [A Thread-Register Decoupled GPU Execution Model for Efficient Tensor Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19628)
*Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yangjie Zhou,Yitong Ding,Guanlin Zhu,Yilu Huang,Chiheng Jin,Chen Zhang,Shixuan Sun,Yu Feng,Anbang Wu,Minyi Guo,Jian Weng,Jiajin Tu,Junsong Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FIBER是一种扩展GPU SIMT模型的新架构，通过将执行实例（fiber）与私有寄存器所有权解耦，实现动态并行度扩展和细粒度寄存器级数据流调度，从而高效编排现代AI工作负载中交织的GEMM和非GEMM操作，在混合精度LLM服务场景下实现2.25x（Ampere）、1.8x（Hopper）和2.09x（Blackwell）的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU将Tensor Core集成到执行管线中，但现有的固定并行度和粗粒度调度机制难以高效编排现代AI工作负载——这些负载在GEMM操作中交织了大量非GEMM操作，导致整个张量计算管线面临显著的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出FIBER架构，其核心执行实例"fiber"与私有寄存器所有权解耦，仅携带最小控制状态，通过SM寄存器的共享视图进行访问。该架构通过扩展ISA、微架构和编译器，实现共享寄存器寻址、无冲突操作数交付和基于fiber的程序映射，从而支持动态并行度缩放和细粒度寄存器级数据流调度，并提供无冗余的矩阵操作数供应。

Result: 在典型混合精度LLM服务场景下，FIBER在Ampere上实现2.25x端到端加速（原始FP16计算为1.15x），在Hopper和Blackwell上分别实现1.8x和2.09x加速，内核级性能提升最高达2.49x。

Conclusion: FIBER通过将执行实例从私有寄存器所有权中解耦，突破了传统GPU SIMT模型中固定并行度和粗粒度调度的限制，为现代AI工作负载下的张量计算编排提供了高效的解决方案，且该方案在跨代GPU架构上均展现出显著的性能收益。

Abstract: Modern GPUs increasingly integrate Tensor Cores into the execution pipeline. Although aggregate tensor throughput continues to grow, aided by an operand supply that has evolved from register-based in Ampere to redundancy-free, memory-based in Hopper and Blackwell, efficiently orchestrating the complete tensor compute pipeline for the modern AI workloads remains challenging. We identify the fundamental bottlenecks as fixed parallelism and coarse-grained scheduling, both of which are exposed by modern AI workloads that interleave diverse non-GEMM operations with GEMM. To orchestrate tensor computation efficiently, we propose FIBER, a new architecture that extends the GPU SIMT (single instruction, multiple thread) model. Its basic execution instance, the \emph{fiber}, is decoupled from private register ownership, carrying only minimal control state while accessing an SM's registers through a shared view. This enables dynamic parallelism scaling, fine-grained register-level dataflow scheduling, and offers a redundancy-free alternative for matrix operand supply. We extend the ISA, microarchitecture, and compiler to realize shared-register addressing, conflict-free operand delivery, and fiber-based program mapping. Under a typical mixed-precision LLM serving scenario, FIBER achieves a 2.25x end-to-end speedup on Ampere (1.15x for the original FP16 computation), with 1.8x and 2.09x on Hopper and Blackwell respectively, and kernel-level gains up to 2.49x.

</details>


### [155] [Automated Estimation of MBIST Area and Test Time in Heterogeneous Memory IPs via Stacked Ensemble Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19705)
*Chee Jin Teoh,Ab Al-Hadi Ab Rahman,Johnny Kee Hui Wong,Premkumar A/L Kesavan Prabagaran,Muhammad Nadzir Marsono,Nuzhat Khan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于监督学习的框架，直接从RTL级设计参数预测MBIST面积和测试时间开销，无需综合。采用多项式扩展、对数变换和缩放后的堆叠集成模型（XGBoost、LightGBM、神经网络+梯度提升元学习器，以及XGBoost/LightGBM+Ridge回归），在±10%误差范围内分别达到90.68%和96.80%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式存储器在现代SoC设计中占用大量面积，MBIST虽保障存储器可靠性但引入额外面积和测试时间开销。传统方法依赖完整RTL综合和测试向量生成，速度慢且资源密集。设计规划阶段需要快速且准确的开销预估方法，以支持更高效的设计决策。

Method: 采用监督学习框架，从RTL级设计参数（存储器数量、字宽、地址深度、端口配置、时钟域）直接预测MBIST面积和测试时间。使用Synopsys Design Compiler和MINT工具生成4,470个面积样本和624个测试时间样本。面积预测采用多项式扩展、对数变换和缩放预处理，结合XGBoost、LightGBM和神经网络构成堆叠集成模型，以梯度提升作为元学习器；测试时间预测采用XGBoost和LightGBM结合Ridge回归，通过100次Optuna搜索进行超参数调优。

Result: 面积预测在±10%误差范围内达到90.68%准确率，测试时间预测达到96.80%准确率，相比基线方法分别提升8.53%和48.80%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了对MBIST成本和测试时间的快速预估，无需进行完整综合，显著提升了早期设计规划的效率和准确性，有助于在存储器IP开发中做出更优设计决策。

Abstract: Embedded memories occupy a large portion of modern System-on-Chip (SoC) designs, especially in high-performance applications such as artificial intelligence and edge computing. Memory Built-In Self-Test (MBIST) is commonly used to ensure memory reliability, but it introduces additional area and test time overhead. Accurate early estimation of these overheads is important during design planning, yet conventional methods rely on full Register Transfer Level (RTL) synthesis and test pattern generation, which are slow and resource-intensive. This study proposes a supervised learning framework that predicts MBIST area and test time directly from RTL-level design parameters without synthesis. A dataset of 4,470 samples for area and 624 for test time was generated using Synopsys Design Compiler and MINT, an Intel-enhanced MBIST tool. Input features include memory count, word width, address depth, port configuration, and clock domains. For area prediction, the features are processed through polynomial expansion, log transformation, and scaling, followed by a stacked ensemble model using XGBoost, LightGBM, and a Neural Network with Gradient Boosting as the meta-learner. For test time, XGBoost and LightGBM are combined using Ridge Regression, with hyperparameters tuned through a 100-trial Optuna search. The models achieved 90.68% accuracy for area and 96.80% for test time within a +/-10% margin, improving over baseline methods by 8.53% and 48.80% respectively. The results show that this approach enables faster estimation of MBIST costs and supports more efficient design decisions in memory IP development.

</details>


### [156] [Energy-Efficient Visual Inspection with FFT-Based CNNs and Adaptive Floating-Point Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19837)
*Lukas Krupp,Marco Groß,Michael Graichen,Kim Ulrich,Norbert Wehn*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在工业CPU-FPGA平台上使用降精度浮点运算进行基于FFT的CNN推理。结合FFT卷积与自适应训练后FP8量化，提出了渐进偏置调整（PBA）和逐层指数偏置选择两种FPGA优化方法，在LeNet-5加速器上实现并评估，最终将准确率从80.33%提升至84.13%，相比纯CPU推理能效提升约2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业CPU-FPGA异构平台上，基于FFT的CNN推理虽然能利用频域卷积降低计算复杂度，但浮点运算的资源开销和功耗较高。需要通过降精度浮点量化（如FP8）来提升FPGA上的推理效率，同时保持模型精度，实现精度与能效的权衡。

Method: 1) 将FFT卷积与自适应训练后FP8量化相结合；2) 提出渐进偏置调整（PBA）方法用于FFT内部降低量化误差；3) 提出逐层指数偏置选择方法用于跨CNN层优化量化策略；4) 使用串行radix-2^2 SDF FFT模块在LeNet-5加速器上实现上述方法，并在工业故障检测数据集上评估。

Result: 实验结果表明：权重缩放方法优于PBA方法；逐层偏置优化在不修改数据通路宽度的前提下将准确率从80.33%提升至84.13%；相比纯CPU推理，FPGA实现了约2.5倍的能量效率提升。

Conclusion: 在CPU-FPGA平台上，通过逐层指数偏置选择和权重缩放等自适应FP8量化策略，能够在保持高精度的同时大幅提升能效。PBA在FFT内部的效果不如权重缩放，说明量化误差的主要来源更倾向于权重而非FFT计算过程中的偏置累积。

Abstract: This paper investigates reduced-precision floating-point arithmetic for FFT-based CNN inference on an industrial CPU-FPGA platform. We combine FFT-based convolution with adaptive post-training FP8 quantization and evaluate two FPGA-oriented optimization methods: progressive bias adjustment (PBA) within the FFT and layer-wise exponent-bias selection across the CNN. The methods are implemented in a LeNet-5 accelerator using serial radix-$2^2$ SDF FFT modules and evaluated on an industrial fault detection dataset. Results show that weight scaling outperforms PBA, while layer-wise bias optimization increases the accuracy from 80.33% to 84.13% without modifying the datapath width. Compared with CPU-only inference, the FPGA achieves approximately 2.5$\times$ higher energy efficiency.

</details>


### [157] [Experimental Verification of Fast Voltage Droop Correction Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19954)
*Shreyas Srinivas,Ian W. Jones,Carsten Schulze,Milos Krstic,Christoph Lenzen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于IHP 130nm工艺的全数字快速自适应电压跌落（voltage droop）响应电路的实现，并在测试芯片上实验验证了其功能。该电路通过动态调整时钟频率来应对电压跌落，解决了检测和存储电压跌落状态时可能出现的亚稳态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着能效优化趋势下保护带（guard band）逐渐缩小，电压跌落成为现代VLSI电路工作频率的关键限制因素。动态适应时钟频率虽可缓解此问题，但用于检测和存储电压跌落状态的器件存在亚稳态挑战，需要提出有效的解决方案。

Method: 该设计实现了一个全数字的快速自适应电路，用于对电压跌落作出应答性响应。设计基于IHP 130nm工艺实现，具体设计描述见配套论文。本文通过测试芯片对设计进行了实验验证。

Result: 在测试芯片上成功实验验证了该全数字自适应电压跌落响应电路的功能。

Conclusion: 所提出的全数字电路能够有效应对电压跌落，通过实验验证了其在实际芯片上的可行性和正确性，为缩小保护带和节能设计提供了支持。

Abstract: Due to the trend towards minimizing guard bands for energy saving purposes, voltage droops are a key limiting factor for the operational frequency of today's VLSI circuits. Adapting clock frequencies dynamically presents the challenge of metastability in the device that detects and stores the existence of voltage droops. We present an implementation of a fast all-digital circuit for adaptive response to droops using the IHP 130 nm process. The description of the design is presented in an accompanying paper. We experimentally validate the functionality of the design on a test chip.

</details>


### [158] [ODEONN: A Digital ODE Solver Architecture for Oscillatory Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20110)
*Bram F. Haverkort,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ODEONN，一种模块化、可扩展的通用数字振荡神经网络架构，支持复值耦合，并通过正弦函数近似实现硬件资源节省一半，相比软件仿真性能退化小于2%，能效延迟积降低45倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡神经网络（ONNs）是AI和组合优化问题的替代计算范式，但现有的数字架构通常针对ONN的特定应用设计，缺乏通用性。因此需要一种适用于ONN多种应用场景的模块化、可扩展的通用数字架构。

Method: 提出名为ODEONN的全数字模块化可扩展架构，是第一款支持复值耦合的全数字ONN。同时引入一种正弦函数的近似方法，相比标准方法节省一半的硬件资源。

Result: ODEONN与全精度软件仿真相比，性能退化小于2%；在能量延迟积方面，相比在传统硬件上运行的软件仿真降低了45倍。

Conclusion: 定点量化和近似波形对计算精度影响很小，ODEONN在保持高精度的同时实现了显著的能效提升，证明了其作为通用数字ONN架构的可行性。

Abstract: Oscillatory Neural Networks (ONNs) are an alternative computing paradigm for AI and combinatorial optimization problems. However, digital architectures are often designed for specific applications of ONNs. This work introduces a modular and scalable architecture called ODEONN that is generic to multiple applications of ONNs, and to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully digital ONN to also support complex-valued coupling. Additionally, an approximation of the sine function is introduced that uses half of the hardware resources compared to standard methods. The performance of ODEONN is compared with a full-precision software simulation, where a performance degradation of less than $2\%$ is shown. Therefore, we conclude that the fixed-point quantization and the approximated waveform affect the accuracy of computation by only a small amount. Furthermore, ODEONN shows a 45$\times$ reduction in energy-delay product over the software simulation running on conventional hardware.

</details>


### [159] [A Resource-Efficient CNN-Based EEG Auditory Attention Decoding ASIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20198)
*Qier Ma,Richard George,Stefan Scholze,Jehn Constantin,Tobias Reichenbach,Christian Mayr*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款面向实时EEG听觉注意解码（AAD）的资源高效ASIC，集成量化CNN推理引擎和Pearson相关分类器，采用流式执行、片上缓冲和内存高效数据流降低硬件成本。芯片在GF22FDX 22nm CMOS工艺下实现，总面积2.09 mm²，0.55V供电下功耗仅0.4941 mW，推理延迟7.34 ms，为助听应用提供高能效硬件平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鸡尾酒会问题对人工耳蜗（CI）用户在嘈杂环境中跟踪目标说话人尤为困难。近年来基于EEG的听觉注意解码（AAD）结合神经网络的方法被探索用于增强助听功能，但缺乏资源高效、可实时运行的硬件实现方案。

Method: 提出一种资源高效的ASIC架构，集成量化CNN推理引擎和Pearson相关分类器。采用流式执行（streaming execution）、片上缓冲（on-chip buffering）和内存高效数据流（memory-efficient dataflow）策略以降低硬件成本，同时维持实时推理性能。

Result: 芯片在GF22FDX 22-nm CMOS工艺下完全实现，总硅片面积2.09 mm²（1264μm × 1654μm），其中CNN推理引擎和流式分类引擎仅占0.076 mm²。在0.55V核心电压下，功耗为0.4941 mW，推理延迟7.34 ms。

Conclusion: 该设计为EEG基于的听觉注意解码在助听应用中的实时部署提供了能量高效的硬件平台，验证了量化和内存高效架构设计在超低功耗边缘场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Following a target speaker in a noisy environment, commonly known as the cocktail party problem, remains particularly challenging for cochlear implant (CI) users. Recent studies have explored EEG-based auditory attention decoding (AAD) using neural networks to enhance hearing assistance. This paper presents a resource-efficient ASIC for real-time EEG-based auditory attention decoding by integrating a quantized CNN inference engine and a Pearson-correlation classifier. The proposed architecture employs streaming execution, on-chip buffering, and memory-efficient dataflow to reduce hardware cost while maintaining real-time performance.
  The proposed ASIC has been fully implemented in GF22FDX 22-nm CMOS technology, occupying a total silicon area of 2.09 mm$^2$(1264$μ$m x 1654$μ$m), with the CNN inference engine and streaming classification engine requiring only 0.076 mm$^2$. Operating at a core voltage of 0.55 V, the design achieves a power consumption of 0.4941 mW and an inference latency of 7.34 ms, providing an energy-efficient hardware platform for EEG-based auditory attention decoding in hearing-assistance applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于监督机器学习的直升机起飞重量估计模型，利用空中客车全球在役机队的大规模数据，遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准，采用长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络实现，并在传统航电计算机上验证了其适合机载部署，用于关键功能如机载告警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量是飞行安全的关键参数，直接影响机载告警等关键功能的准确性。传统的重量估计方法精度有限，需要一种更精确、可部署于机载环境且符合航空适航标准的机器学习模型来解决这一问题。

Method: 采用监督机器学习方法，基于空中客车全球在役机队的大规模数据集，构建LSTM循环神经网络作为重量估计模型；同时提出一套机器学习需求（ML Requirements）和机器学习模型描述（ML Model Description），并遵循EASA概念文件与Eurocae ED-324标准进行学习保证流程，最后在传统航电计算机（legacy avionics computers）上对需求进行验证。

Result: 在传统航电计算机上成功验证了所提出的机器学习需求在实现上的满足情况，结果表明该LSTM重量估计模型适用于机载部署，能够支撑起飞阶段的关键功能如机载告警。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了机器学习在航空关键功能中的应用可行性与合规路径，为机器学习模型在机载环境的适航认证与部署提供了参考框架和实用案例。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [161] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的距离度量——三角模糊数重缩放距离（d_TR），该度量将线性重缩放直接集成到距离计算中，解决了传统模糊距离度量在处理异质属性（不同尺度或单位）时需要额外归一化步骤的问题。论文形式化证明了d_TR满足度量属性（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），且具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，适用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策分析等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的决策经常涉及不精确或不确定的信息，常以模糊集（特别是三角模糊数TFNs）表示。许多模糊方法需要量化TFNs之间的距离，但现有距离度量假设所有值处于同一尺度，在处理具有不同尺度或单位的异质属性时需要预先进行归一化。这一预处理步骤增加了复杂性，且可能导致信息损失或结果偏差，因此需要一种能够直接在距离计算中实现归一化的度量方法。

Method: 提出三角模糊重缩放距离（d_TR），其核心创新是将线性重缩放（LRE）直接集成到距离计算过程中，使得模糊数比较时自动完成归一化，无需单独的预处理阶段。论文通过数学证明验证d_TR满足度量的四个基本性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并进一步证明其有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。此外，该方法支持引入权重向量以优先考虑不同维度的重要性。

Result: 从理论层面证明了d_TR是一种有效的度量（metric），满足非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式等全部度量性质，同时具备有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性等优良特性。这些性质保证了该度量在异质模糊数据场景下的数学严谨性和实际适用性。

Conclusion: d_TR为处理异质模糊数据提供了一个方法论上的改进，通过在距离计算中内嵌归一化机制，消除了传统方法对预归一化步骤的依赖。其结合权重向量的能力使其在合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等领域具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [162] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Holtercare-23K，一个大规模多模态动态心电图数据集，包含22,980对问答对，来自788份临床Holter记录，并引入信号-视频-文本三模态对齐方案。基于此构建Holtercare-Bench基准，评估MLLMs在时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结任务上的表现。零样本评估显示现有MLLMs在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距，而微调代表性模型可带来实质改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在医学应用中表现出色，但大多偏向静态图像或短期信号，在动态心电图中难以处理复杂的时间推理和诊断报告生成，主要原因是缺乏高质量数据集和基准。

Method: (i) 构建Holtercare-23K大规模多模态动态ECG数据集，包含22,980个QA对，源自788份临床Holter记录，提出信号-视频-文本三模态对齐机制；(ii) 提出Holtercare-Bench基准，涵盖时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个评估维度。

Result: 零样本评估表明主流MLLMs在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后性能获得显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前MLLMs在心电生理学领域的局限性，并为长期医学MLLMs提供了基础性基准。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [163] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了量子-经典混合机器学习方法用于基于cfDNA片片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测，在20和40特征子集上使用多种量子核方法（角度编码+不同纠缠策略），并与经典SVM基线比较。片段组学数据上多个量子核模型优于经典SVM，甲基化数据上经典SVM最佳但部分量子模型仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌筛查（低剂量CT）能降低死亡率，但受限于依从性和管理挑战。血液cfDNA生物标志物作为补充手段，但肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测困难，需要更强大的机器学习方法来捕捉这些复杂模式。

Method: 采用量子-经典混合机器学习框架：先进行特征选择（20和40特征子集），将特征通过角度编码（angle和dense-angle特征映射）结合多种纠缠策略编码到量子希尔伯特空间，使用精确statevector模拟计算基于保真度的量子核，再与预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归集成，并与基于原始特征训练的SVM基线对比。

Result: 在重复的留出集评估中，量子核模型在两个数据集上均达到竞争性性能。片段组学数据上多个20特征配置相比经典SVM基线提升了AUC；甲基化数据上经典SVM获得最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具竞争力且部分情况提高特异性。特征数从20增至40并未一致提升性能，反而常增加变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法在cfDNA肺癌检测中展现出前景，系统评估了编码和纠缠设计对分类的影响，支持量子-经典混合方法作为该领域的可行探索方向，同时提示特征数选择需谨慎。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [164] [Decoding silent reading from non-invasive EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20186)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从脑电图（EEG）信号中非侵入性解码内心言语（inner speech）的可行性。由于无法收集与自发内心独白配对的大脑活动数据，作者将默读（silent reading）作为可扩展的代理任务，让一名参与者接受约49小时、24万个单词的EEG记录，采用CLIP式对比学习目标训练卷积编码器（可选用因果Transformer），实现了基于开放词表的词汇级解码，并发现解码性能随数据量呈对数线性增长、未见饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内心言语的非侵入性解码面临根本性数据问题：无法收集与自发内心独白配对的大脑信号数据，现有代理范式（线索重复式与回顾报告式生成性内心言语）采集缓慢、时间锁定性差且难以验证受试者依从性。研究者希望找到一种可扩展的代理范式来建立脑活动与词汇/语义信息之间的映射，从而推动内心言语解码研究。

Method: 1）将默读作为可扩展的代理任务，使用快速序列视觉呈现（RSVP）展示连续叙事文本中的单词，并对每个单词的排版进行随机化以部分去相关词身份与低级视觉形式；2）使用19通道干电极EEG对单个密集采样参与者进行约49小时的记录（240,000词展示，393个运行）；3）训练卷积EEG编码器（可选接因果Transformer），采用CLIP式对比目标将短时EEG窗口与大语言模型生成的词隐藏状态嵌入对齐；4）通过词分组top-10检索评估解码性能，并与置换基线比较。

Result: 解码性能可靠高于机会水平，扩展到中频和罕见词，随训练数据量呈对数线性增长且无饱和迹象。移除枕叶和后颞叶电极使词级增益下降约三分之一，但上下文追踪不受影响。对照分析将词级解码与叙事上下文追踪及Transformer位置嵌入引入的非神经位置先验区分开来。

Conclusion: 研究确立了开放词表词汇级信息可从默读期间的EEG中恢复，且当前解码性能受限于数据量而非饱和。这为内心言语解码提供了一种可扩展的代理范式，未来可通过更多数据进一步改善解码性能。

Abstract: Non-invasive decoding of inner speech faces a fundamental data problem: a corpus pairing brain activity with a person's spontaneous inner monologue cannot be collected, and the available proxy paradigms (cued repetitive and retrospectively reported generative inner speech) are slow to acquire, poorly time-locked, and subject compliance is unverifiable. We therefore treat silent reading as a scalable proxy task and ask how much lexical and semantic information a contrastive decoder can extract from it. We report an open-vocabulary analysis of approximately 240,000 word presentations recorded from a single densely-sampled participant across 393 runs (ca. 49 h) of 19-channel dry-electrode EEG. Words from continuous narrative text were presented in rapid serial visual presentation, with typography randomised on every trial to partially decorrelate word identity from low-level visual form. A convolutional EEG encoder, optionally followed by a causal transformer, was trained with a CLIP-style contrastive objective to align short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model. Decoding, evaluated as word-grouped top-10 retrieval against permutation baselines, was reliably above chance, extended to mid-frequency and rare words, and scaled log-linearly with training-data volume with no sign of saturation. Removing occipital and posterior-temporal electrodes reduced the word-level gain by roughly one third but left context tracking unchanged. Control analyses separate word-level decoding from narrative context tracking and from a non-neural positional prior introduced by the transformer's positional embedding. These results establish that open-vocabulary word-level information is recoverable from EEG during silent reading, and that decoding is data-limited rather than saturated.

</details>


### [165] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)框架，利用纵向贝叶斯学习从diffusion tensor imaging (DTI)中连续估计阿尔茨海默病(AD)的疾病严重程度，输出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)作为个体在AD连续谱上的量化位置及不确定性估计，在ADNI数据集上优于现有疾病进展方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD是一个连续的生物学进展过程，但现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于横断面影像上的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续发展轨迹，缺乏对疾病进程中时间动态和不确定性的建模。

Method: DCP采用纵向贝叶斯学习框架，将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量，联合整合纵向观测与弱临床监督信息，从而推导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)来量化个体在AD连续谱上的位置及其伴随的不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明，DCP在代表性疾病进展方法中表现一致最优。综合验证分析显示DCS能准确表征疾病严重程度、具有较强临床相关性、保持纵向疾病演化规律，并能预测未来疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了一种定量的影像衍生表征，可实现超越传统诊断标签和临床评分的阿尔茨海默病进展连续评估，为AD的个体化连续监测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [166] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用目标数据集来提升大语言模型（LLM）不确定性量化（UQ）性能的方法：通过构建简单分类器，将现有零样本不确定性分数与相似查询的正确性作为特征，预测LLM响应的正确性，从而在几乎无额外计算开销的情况下持续超越现有的零样本UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性量化对于LLM的安全部署至关重要。现有方法（如语言化置信度、多代生成方法）大多是零样本的，虽然无需标注数据就能产生不确定性分数，但在实际部署前必须评估其在目标数据集上的性能。作者指出，既然可以利用这个数据集进行评估，为什么不进一步利用它来提升UQ性能。

Method: 构建简单分类器，以现有UQ分数和相似查询的正确性作为特征，预测LLM响应是否正确。该方法在部署前利用目标数据集对分类器进行训练，核心创新在于将UQ分数与基于相似性的正确性信息相结合，作为分类特征。

Result: 该方法在多个数据集上持续优于现有的零样本UQ分数方法，且计算开销极小，作为现有UQ方法的廉价且直接的增强方案有效。

Conclusion: 利用部署前的数据集来构建基于分类器的UQ增强方法，是一种简单、低开销且有效的策略，可显著提升LLM不确定性量化的性能，适用于实际应用场景。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [167] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层成像"(mechanistic tomography)框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积及子集干预等可解释性测量统一为y=Ax+w的线性测量模型，并给出从低成本测量到逐步扩展测量家族的实用流程。在控制任务上验证了观测器误差与控制器误差的耦合，并在多组模型实验（双HMM、Tracr、GPT-2-small IOI、Qwen-2.5-7B）中检验该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性需要恢复模型未直接暴露的量（如表征状态、组件效应、交互与干预响应），但现有方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-vector积、子集干预）在访问假设、目标量与测量代价上各不相同，缺乏统一的共享测度结构描述，导致方法选择与误差诊断缺乏指导原则。

Method: 提出mechanistic tomography框架：对选定基与干预族，将测量统一形式化为y=Ax+w，其中A为干预设计矩阵，x为目标映射，w为非线性响应、采样误差与基误设项。给出实用流程：(1)从最低成本测量开始；(2)在目标尺度下的留出干预上测试；(3)校准简单失配；(4)当存在结构化残差时扩展测量族。以控制任务作为严格验证场景（估计量充当观测器）。

Result: 在双HMM模型中，控制误差随观测器误差上升，而目标改善可能掩盖干扰状态移动；在仅前向访问下，稀疏聚合测量能以比坐标patching更少的干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下有限探测可改进局部归因映射；提升测量与Hessian-vector积能恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互，而Tracr显示所需测量族取决于基的选择。在GPT-2-small IOI上，Name Mover与Negative Name Mover间的交互是三个跨组对中最大的留出预测项；在Qwen-2.5-7B上，有限校准使加性拒绝响应映射充分，留出误差不支持成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制断层成像提供了一个统一的测量设计语言，将现有可解释性方法纳入一个可迭代校准的框架；不同模型、不同基与不同访问假设下所需测量族不同，应依据留出干预误差驱动的自适应流程选择测量方案，而非预先固定方法。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [168] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于模板（template）复用的神经网络鲁棒性验证加速技术进行了系统性研究，提出了新指标"联合稳定神经元"来解释模板子承率在不同场景下的巨大差异，并设计了FastCert技术来自动分配或裁剪模板，在L0验证任务上平均提速1.13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板复用技术已被证明可以通过跨查询重用中间层抽象状态（templates）来加速不完整的神经网络验证，但其在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下是否稳定有效仍未得到充分研究。本文旨在系统性地评估模板加速技术的有效性与局限性。

Method: 首先对模板加速技术进行大规模系统研究，分析模板子承率在不同场景下的变化；其次提出"联合稳定神经元"（jointly stable neurons）这一新指标来解释模板子承率的波动原因；最后提出FastCert技术，自动将模板分配到不同网络层以提升性能影响，并在模板不太可能产生加速时完全弃用模板。

Result: 研究表明模板子承率在不同场景下差异很大，联合稳定神经元指标可以解释这一变化，某些情况下模板技术几乎不可能带来加速。FastCert在大量基于覆盖设计（covering-design）的L0验证任务上，相对于现有模板复用技术实现了平均1.13倍的加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速的有效性高度依赖具体场景，联合稳定神经元可作为预判模板收益的可靠指标；FastCert通过选择性分配模板策略，在保证可验证性的前提下提升了验证效率。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [169] [FleetSieve: Decision-Critical Profiling for SLO-Aware LLM Fleet Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19659)
*Huang Cheng,Scott Zhang,Aubert Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FleetSieve是一种按预期决策影响选择测量配置的方法，用于LLM服务集群中的张量并行度与副本数分配，联合建模容量和尾延迟，在给定容差内达到与穷举profiling接近的最优资源配置，同时大幅减少GPU测量时长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务集群中选择张量并行（TP）度和副本数非常困难：性能关于TP非单调，且可行配置会随负载变化。穷举profiling虽能消除不确定性，但会测量大量对最终资源分配无影响的配置，造成不必要的GPU开销。

Method: FleetSieve根据测量对"资源耦合的SLO感知集群决策"的预期影响来选择测量子集。它联合建模容量与尾延迟，比较保守分配与乐观分配，当两者之间的决策间隙低于指定容差时停止测量。

Result: 在固定H100测量网格下，FleetSieve对31B参数开源模型使用22,200 GPU-秒达到oracle聚合决策，比均匀随机profiling少6.9%；在200个随机揭示顺序下平均节省5.4%（95% bootstrap CI: 3.5-7.2%）。固定比较中Chat场景节省21.5%，Code场景未达到最少GPU-秒。联合容量与尾延迟建模避免了选择46.4秒p99完成时间、违反30秒SLO的配置。在16-GPU分配中，稀疏profiling错误决策损失最多1.93 requests/s和12.4个百分点max-min满足率。

Conclusion: FleetSieve通过按决策影响智能选择测量，在保持SLO感知的资源分配质量的同时显著降低profiling开销，并验证了负载相关的尾延迟机制。

Abstract: Choosing tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and replica counts for an LLM serving fleet is difficult because performance is not monotonic in TP and the feasible choice can change with load. Exhaustive profiling resolves this uncertainty, but measures many configurations that do not affect the final resource allocation. We present FleetSieve, which selects measurements according to their expected effect on a resource-coupled, SLO-aware fleet decision. FleetSieve models capacity and tail latency jointly, compares conservative and optimistic allocations, and stops when their remaining decision gap is below a specified tolerance. On a fixed H100 measurement grid for a 31B-parameter open-weight model, FleetSieve reaches the oracle aggregate decision using 22,200 GPU-seconds, 6.9% less than uniform random profiling in the fixed comparison. Across 200 random reveal orders, its mean saving over random profiling is 5.4% (95% bootstrap CI: 3.5-7.2%). The fixed-comparison saving is 21.5% for Chat, while FleetSieve does not use the fewest GPU-seconds for Code. Joint capacity and tail modeling also avoids selecting a configuration whose 46.4-second completion p99 violates a 30-second SLO. In a 16-GPU allocation, an incorrect sparse-profile decision loses up to 1.93 requests/s and 12.4 percentage points of max-min fulfillment. Boundary repeats and BurstGPT measurements support the observed load-dependent tail-latency mechanism.

</details>


### [170] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EventTime框架，用于预测网络安全事件披露后的短期异常金融损失。该框架结合多分辨率时间序列表示与事件元数据，通过事件融合模块和动态对比学习目标应对稀疏高影响事件与背景噪声的挑战，并在自构建的SECURE真实数据集上超越多种基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件等外生冲击会通过网络传播并突然扰乱金融时间序列，导致显著的异常损失。然而，大多数时间序列预测模型仅关注趋势、季节性和自相关等内生规律，难以处理罕见且异构的外部事件。此外，高影响事件稀疏且背景市场噪声大，进一步放大了预测难度。

Method: EventTime采用多分辨率框架，整合长时程市场上下文、短时程事件前动态和事件元数据。其核心包括：(1)事件融合模块，将时间表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关的近期市场模式；(2)动态对比学习目标，构建事件感知和时间序列感知的正负样本对以缓解稀疏监督问题。同时构建SECURE数据集，将网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列及结构化/LLM衍生语义特征对齐。

Result: 实验表明EventTime在估计事件后金融损失方面一致优于最先进的时间序列和事件感知基线。进一步分析显示其具有更强的事件敏感表示、对不完整元数据更高的鲁棒性，以及更可解释的短期市场影响估计。

Conclusion: EventTime有效解决了事件条件下的罕见外生冲击影响预测问题，为网络安全事件披露后的短期金融影响估计提供了更准确、更鲁棒且更可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [171] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector框架，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力，将表格数据的正常状态知识（统计摘要、因果依赖、蒸馏原型）组织成提示词，生成评分引擎用于异常检测，在24个表格数据集上超越15个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常检测具有挑战性，因为异常样本往往表现为跨特征依赖关系被违反，而非简单的边际偏差。现有检测器依赖几何或重构信号，而先前基于LLM的方法主要对LLM进行正常样本的微调或生成合成异常样本，未能充分利用LLM的上下文学习能力来推导异常检测逻辑。

Method: 将正常训练数据转换为统计摘要、因果依赖和蒸馏原型，组织成提示词用于代码生成，得到的评分引擎综合评估统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常，为每个测试样本计算异常分数。该方法无需LLM微调或神经网络训练。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上，与15个SOTA基线相比，LLM-Detector在混合类型和纯连续型设置下均取得一致的性能提升，同时消除了LLM微调和神经网络训练的需求，降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: LLM-Detector通过结构化的提示条件评分合成，使LLM能够从正常状态知识中自动推导异常检测逻辑，实现了高效、低成本且实用的表格数据异常检测。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [172] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究生产机器学习系统在概念漂移下的模型刷新策略。核心发现：最具影响力的设计决策不是重训策略本身，而是部署模型是否支持增量学习。当采用逐样本增量更新时（针对本研究的线性在线学习器），任何策略与不重训基线在54组配对比较中均无实际显著差异；当不支持增量学习时，策略选择使漂移后准确率相差15-55个百分点，且周期性重训在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于两类反应式策略，反应式策略仅在循环漂移下保留优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会退化，但实践者缺乏何时重训的原则性指导。重训成本高昂、预算有限，且重训的模型不会立即生效——训练和部署延迟会留下过时模型在数据持续移动时提供预测，这一关键问题此前未被充分研究。

Method: 在统一的系统模型下（显式建模重训预算和训练加部署延迟），对三种实际模型刷新策略（周期性重训、误差阈值触发、基于ADWIN的统计漂移触发重训）与不重训基线进行受控经验研究。实验规模为3933次运行，跨越三种漂移机制、三个预算水平、最多五个延迟级别、三个数据集和两种学习模式。

Result: 1) 增量学习下，所有策略与不重训基线在54组配对比较中均无实际显著差异；2) 无增量学习时，策略选择造成15-55个百分点准确率差异，周期性重训在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于反应式策略；3) 反应式策略仅在循环漂移下更有优势；4) 发现了反应式策略的系统性失效模式，以及延迟-预算排队交互会静默地将有效重训预算减半。

Conclusion: 增量学习能力比重训策略选择更为关键；在没有增量学习的情况下，简单周期重训优于反应式重训。研究还揭示了反应式策略的失效模式和延迟-预算排队交互问题，并发布了完整的模拟器、数据管道和逐次运行工件以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [173] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeltaMomentum：一种将方向感知性引入动量更新规则的优化器组件。它利用线性层梯度的键值结构，用规范delta规则更新动量缓冲，使每个方向按其出现频率遗忘。作为任何优化器动量缓冲的即插即用替代品，在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW（AdamW+DeltaMomentum）达到AdamW验证损失所需的步数最多减少46%，且额外计算开销仅为一个门控MLP块线性成本的22.2%-25.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代优化器（如AdamW、SGD等）的动量机制采用指数移动平均（EMA）以固定速率遗忘所有方向，但深度网络在训练中看到的输入呈现出高度各向异性——少数方向被频繁查询而多数方向很少出现。现有方法通过在此缓冲周围添加额外处理来解决这种各向异性，但动量更新本身保持不变。作者希望构建真正具有方向感知性的动量更新规则。

Method: 核心观察：线性层的梯度可分解为作为"键"的输入和作为"值"的输出侧误差。利用这种键值结构，DeltaMomentum使用规范delta规则更新动量缓冲，使每个方向以与其出现频率相关的速率被遗忘。论文证明它：(1)是有效动量；(2)无需矩阵求逆即可应用输入侧曲率校正；(3)在固定和漂移最优解下都能比EMA更快清除陈旧方向。可用作任何优化器动量缓冲的drop-in替代品，系数在μP下可跨宽度迁移。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW在67M规模下达到AdamW验证损失所需步数减少46.39±4.32%，在370M规模下减少22.12±0.80%（三种子）；在1B Chinchilla最优预算下收益持续。与同协议调优的Muon基线相比，DeltaAdamW在两种语言模型规模上均更优。该增益在SGD、ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny（CIFAR-10）上也成立。额外计算仅为门控MLP块线性成本的22.2%-25.0%，无持久内存开销。训练时诊断确认了预测机制：更好的梯度追踪和更健康的输入方向。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum通过将方向感知性直接嵌入动量更新规则（而非在其外包裹额外处理），有效解决了训练输入各向异性带来的问题。它能更快清除陈旧方向、自动应用输入侧曲率校正，在多个模型规模和架构上显著提升收敛效率，且计算开销可控、可无缝替代现有动量缓冲。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Multimodal Alignment: Certifying Physical Language through Response Substitution and Ordered Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19492)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Yixiao Li,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入操作能力层级结构和Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC)框架，检验不同传感器模态是否可互换地承载相同可执行语义。实验表明，在Clustered Haptic任务中音频和加速度表示在响应空间上仅差4.5x于错误配对，且所有19个保留表面均保持此差距；但在有序执行测试中，冻结执行器在预注册预算下无法推进程序，收敛预算下则能执行（oracle NMSE 0.18），并发现执行器本身（而非表征图表）决定了能力门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型测试无法确定不同传感器是否承载相同可执行含义，也无法验证这种含义在新动作组合下是否仍然有效。作者希望建立一个操作能力层级，区分属性访问、响应替换、融合闭合和有序执行等不同层次的能力，并为每个层次提供独立可检验的标准。

Method: 引入DBOSC证书框架，要求独立训练的各模态编译器在训练面板外的证据上可互换地进入冻结的响应图表。在两个系统上验证：Clustered Haptic（同表面音频与加速度表示为4.5x更接近）和受控弹塑性系统（冻结执行器执行有序程序，比较预注册预算与收敛预算下的表现差异）。

Result: 在Clustered Haptic上，同表面跨模态响应比错误表面配对近4.5倍，19个保留表面全部保持该差距。在有序执行测试中，预注册预算下执行器无法推进任何保留程序，但收敛预算下可执行（oracle NMSE 0.18），融合优于单模态，16项注册检查中14项通过。两个失败源于融合信息矩阵的对角限制表现与完整矩阵相当。发射完整程序的执行器比实体盲预测器差38倍。

Conclusion: 跨模态响应替换和有序执行是可分离的、独立可检验的能力。能力门槛由执行器决定而非表征图表本身。压缩和融合本身无法确定未见的组合规律，非可识别性结果解释了这一限制。

Abstract: World models increasingly treat compact multimodal representations as interfaces between perception and physical interaction, yet existing probes do not establish whether different sensors carry the same executable meaning or whether that meaning survives a new action composition. We introduce an operational capability hierarchy and the Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC), which asks whether independently trained modality compilers enter a frozen response chart interchangeably on evidence outside their training panels. On Cluster Haptic, audio and acceleration representations of the same unseen surface are 4.5x closer in response space than wrong-surface pairings, with the gap holding for all 19 held-out surfaces; unsealing withheld responses confirms that every branch predicts the physics better than the population chart. We then test ordered execution in a controlled elastoplastic system with complementary modality blind spots. At the pre-registered budget, the prerequisite refuses the stack because the frozen executor cannot advance even an exact chart coordinate through a held-out program. At a converged budget, the same rank-three chart executes those programs (oracle NMSE 0.18), fusion improves on both modalities, and 14 of 16 registered checks pass; the two failures arise because a diagonal restriction of the fused information matrix performs as well as the full one. Clearing the gate is a property of the executor, not the chart: an executor emitting whole programs instead of shared per-step dynamics is 38x worse than an entity-blind predictor on the same chart. A matching non-identifiability result explains why compression and fusion alone cannot determine an unseen composition law. These results separate attribute access, response substitution, fusion closure, and ordered execution into distinct, separately testable achievements.

</details>


### [175] [Empirical Characterization of Learning Geometry in Hybrid Quantum Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19497)
*Sandra Leticia Juárez-Osorio,Jorge I. Hernandez-Martinez,Jesus Ivan Ruiz-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过结构性对齐的经典基线对比，系统刻画了紧凑型混合量子预测模型的学习动力学，发现尽管两者在NTK（神经正切核）诊断指标上存在显著差异（经典模型早期目标对齐更强，混合模型核谱更分散且核漂移更小），但在测试集上二者表现相当，且混合模型参数更少（125 vs 281）并在18个频率条件下有15个更早到达验证检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究往往仅以端点精度（endpoint accuracy）衡量量子模型的优劣，忽略了量子与经典模型在学习动态过程中的本质差异。作者旨在揭示架构依赖的学习行为——这些行为在单纯比较最终精度时会被掩盖，从而更全面地理解量子混合模型在优化和泛化上的特性，而非简单宣称量子优势。

Method: 采用结构对齐的经典基线（structurally aligned classical baseline）与紧凑混合量子预测模型进行对比；使用平稳谐波混合（stationary harmonic-mixture）和非平稳线性调频（nonstationary chirp）两种基准，通过控制频谱复杂度和数据量来系统研究。具体工具为经验神经正切核（empirical NTK）分析，包括核-目标对齐（kernel-target alignment）、核漂移（kernel drift）、谱集中度（spectral concentration）和训练损失四个维度。此外还设置了傅里叶增强经典基线和受控数据重加（re-uploading）消融实验。

Result: （1）经典模型表现出更强的早期核-目标对齐，而混合模型的核谱集中度更低、核漂移更小；（2）尽管优化几何不同，两种架构在所有评估场景下达到相似的留出集性能；（3）混合模型仅用125个可训练参数（经典基线为281个），且18个频率条件中有15个更早达到验证检查点；（4）傅里叶增强经典基线无法复现混合模型的训练行为，重上传消融证实重复编码会系统性改变优化和核几何；（5)单一NTK诊断指标不能作为验证收敛的单调预测器。

Conclusion: 可比的泛化性能可以从截然不同的学习轨迹中涌现，体系结构决定的学习行为仅靠端点精度无法被察觉。作者不主张普遍的量子优势，而是指出架构相关的学习动力学差异，强调需要超越最终精度指标来全面评估量子混合模型的特性。

Abstract: We characterize the learning dynamics of a compact hybrid quantum forecasting model through comparison with a structurally aligned classical baseline. Using stationary harmonic-mixture and nonstationary chirp benchmarks with controlled spectral complexity and data availability, we analyze empirical Neural Tangent Kernel dynamics through kernel-target alignment, kernel drift, spectral concentration, and training loss. The classical model exhibits stronger early target alignment, whereas the hybrid model generally develops a less concentrated kernel spectrum and smaller kernel drift. Despite these distinct optimization geometries, both architectures attain similar held-out performance across the evaluated regimes. Notably, the hybrid model uses 125 trainable parameters compared with 281 for the classical baseline and reaches its validation-selected checkpoint earlier in 15 of 18 frequency conditions. A Fourier-augmented classical baseline does not reproduce the observed training behavior, while a controlled re-uploading ablation shows that repeated encoding systematically modifies both optimization and kernel geometry. These results demonstrate that comparable generalization can emerge from substantially different learning trajectories and that individual NTK diagnostics do not provide monotonic predictors of validation convergence. Rather than claiming a general quantum advantage, the study identifies architecture-dependent learning behavior that is masked by endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [176] [In Two Minds about Lifelong Learning: Exploring Hemispheric Redundancy and Specialisation in Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19514)
*Benjamin Smith,Levin Kuhlmann,Kaushik Roy,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出4MAS宏架构，通过模拟生物系统中的体验重放、REM睡眠和大脑半球不对称性，解决机器学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在Split-MNIST、Split-Fashion-MNIST和Split-CIFAR-100数据集上分别达到98.3%、84.9%和29.29%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的机器学习算法在持续学习方面面临重大挑战，通常需要在"保留已有知识"和"适应新数据模式"之间进行权衡。缺乏持续学习能力时，算法必须用整个数据集重新训练，而原始训练数据可能因存储限制、计算成本或隐私约束而不可用。相比之下，生物系统能够持续学习而不发生灾难性遗忘，因此借鉴生物学习机制构建持续学习框架具有重要价值。

Method: 本文构建了一个高层次的生物学习与知识保留框架，重点关注三个概念：体验重放、REM睡眠和大脑双侧化（bilaterality）。在此基础上提出4MAS宏架构，该架构包含四个模块，通过不对称的两个半球，每个半球各自拥有独立的长短期记忆机制，并在增量学习任务之间引入睡眠阶段以促进记忆巩固。

Result: 4MAS架构在Split-MNIST上达到98.3%的准确率，在Split-Fashion-MNIST上达到84.9%的准确率，在Split-CIFAR-100上达到29.29%的准确率，取得了具有竞争力的持续学习结果。

Conclusion: 通过模拟生物大脑的对称性结构（不对称半球及各自的长短期记忆）以及睡眠期间（尤其是REM睡眠）的记忆巩固机制，4MAS架构证明了将生物学原理引入机器学习持续学习设计的可行性，为构建持久智能系统提供了一种新的宏架构思路。

Abstract: Persistent intelligent systems require the ability to learn continually, but current machine learning approaches face significant challenges in this area compared to biological learning systems. Machine learning algorithms typically trade off retention of previously learned information and adaptation to new or changing data patterns. When continual learning capabilities are absent, algorithms must undergo retraining using the entire data set, an approach that becomes impractical when original training data are unavailable due to storage constraints, financial or computational costs, or privacy restrictions. However, biological animals can learn continually, without experiencing catastrophic forgetting. This paper attempts to build a high-level framework for how animals learn and preserve knowledge by modelling neural components and states that are known to be related to memory consolidation. We focus on three concepts: experience replay, REM sleep, and bilaterality. We propose 4MAS (4 Module Awake/Sleep), a novel macroarchitecture demonstrating how machine learning models might benefit from asymmetric hemispheres, each with their own long- and short-term memory mechanisms, and how a period of sleep between incremental learning tasks might benefit memory consolidation. Finally, we present results showing that our architecture achieves competitive results on the Split-MNIST, Split-Fashion-MNIST and Split-CIFAR-100 datasets, with 98.3%, 84.9%, and 29.29% accuracy respectively.

</details>


### [177] [Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19540)
*Yara Bahram,Zahra Dehghani,Mélodie Desbos,Eric Granger,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MeanFlow-Transfer（MF-T）框架，将异构预训练扩散/流模型的输出统一映射到共享的速度表示空间，通过对齐的MF目标在小样本目标域上同时完成适应与加速；并引入连续对抗MeanFlow（CAMF）后训练阶段，将对抗精炼从瞬时速度扩展到有限区间平均速度，在125×更少的NFE下匹配或超越微调教师模型的FID/FDD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个核心痛点：(1) 将预训练扩散/流模型适应到新领域时，昂贵的多步采样问题未被解决，且现有加速方法依赖于源模型的特定参数化（ε、x、v或u），异构预训练模型缺乏共同的加速目标；(2) 对抗精炼虽被证明对少步生成有效，但仅针对瞬时速度流（如Rectified Flow），不适用于MeanFlow（MF）模型所预测的有限区间平均速度。

Method: 分两个阶段：(1) MeanFlow-Transfer：将异构源模型输出（ε、x、v、u）映射到共享的MF速度表示，用源权重初始化MF生成器，在目标域上优化MF目标函数，统一适应与加速于单一训练循环；(2) Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow（CAMF）：扩展连续对抗流模型，将判别准则从瞬时速度推广到MF的有限区间平均速度，通过在真实与预测区间端点之间的学习势能变化进行对比，恢复MF回归平均所丢失的细节，并在区间趋近于零时退化为瞬时准则。

Result: 将四种基于ImageNet的源模型（DiT-ε、SiT-v、JiT-x、iMF-u）适应到五个目标域：MF-T配合CAMF在最多125×更少的NFE下，FID和FDD指标匹配或超越微调教师模型；CAMF平均提升了MF-T少步生成的FID达29%。

Conclusion: MF-T+CAMF提供了一种统一且通用的框架，能够跨异构预训练模型同时实现领域适应和采样加速，对抗精炼成功扩展至有限区间平均速度的MeanFlow范式，在极少数函数评估下达到高质量少步生成效果。

Abstract: Training fast generators on new domains with limited data remains challenging for two reasons. First, adapting a pretrained diffusion or flow model to a new domain leaves its costly multi-step sampling unaddressed, and existing acceleration methods are tied to the source parameterization--$ε$, $x$, $v$, or $u$--leaving heterogeneous pretrained models with no common acceleration target. Second, while adversarial refinement is proven effective for few-step quality, it is formulated only for instantaneous-velocity flows, not for the finite-interval average velocities that MeanFlow (MF) models predict. We address both problems. We propose MeanFlow-Transfer, which maps heterogeneous source outputs into a shared velocity representation, uses it to initialize an MF generator from the source weights, and optimizes an MF objective on the target domain. This unifies adaptation and acceleration in a single training loop across a broad range of pretrained models. We then introduce Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow, a post-training stage that extends continuous adversarial flow models from instantaneous velocities to MF's finite-interval average velocities. CAMF contrasts changes in a learned potential between real and predicted interval endpoints, recovering fine detail that MF regression averages away, and reduces to the instantaneous criterion in the vanishing-interval limit. Adapting four ImageNet-based source models--DiT ($ε$), SiT ($v$), JiT ($x$), iMF ($u$)--to five target domains, MF-T with CAMF matches or exceeds the fine-tuned teacher in FID and FDD at up to $125\times$ fewer Neural Function Evaluations (NFEs), while CAMF improves MF-T's few-step FID by $29\%$ on average.

</details>


### [178] [DraftFM: A FoundationModel for Day-Zero Drafting in Magic: The Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19568)
*Brian Ward*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DraftFM是一个离散选择模型，用于《万智牌》扩展包在发布前（day-zero）的轮抽预测。模型仅使用卡牌的公开特征和文本嵌入（无卡牌身份、系列身份或使用统计），在1.49亿人类选牌数据上训练后，对三个完全留出的扩展包预测准确率达50.8%-60.4%。模型还被用于在《霍比特人》系列正式上线前约36小时生成卡牌排名，并与六位专家评审的一致性相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 万智牌新扩展包发布时，完整卡牌列表已公开但轮抽日志尚不存在，导致有监督的选牌模型无法在新卡牌上直接训练。作者希望研究这种"day-zero"场景，即模型仅凭公开卡牌信息就能对未见过的卡牌做出合理预测，而非依赖历史使用数据。

Method: DraftFM是一个离散选择策略模型，逐张精确评分当前卡包中可用卡牌，以已轮抽的卡池和轮抽状态为条件。每张卡以775维冻结向量表示，包括结构化特征和固定的文本嵌入。模型仅含160万参数，在29个扩展包的1.49亿人类选牌上训练。关键设计是排除卡牌身份、系列身份和使用统计，确保未见卡牌与熟悉卡牌走相同的评分机制。

Result: 模型在三个完全留出的扩展包上分别达到50.8%、60.4%和56.7%的top-1一致率，而开局选牌的均匀随机基线约为7%。模型产生的《霍比特人》系列卡牌排名与六位独立专家评审的一致性程度，与专家之间彼此的一致性相当。

Conclusion: DraftFM证明了在没有任何历史轮抽数据的情况下，仅基于公开卡牌文本和结构特征就能有效预测人类选牌行为。这一day-zero能力使模型能在新集发布前即提供有意义的卡牌排名，并已在实际部署中通过加密溯源保障了预测的可验证性。对实际结果的评估将在后续笔记中公布。

Abstract: Drafting a new Magic: The Gathering expansion begins before any pick from it has been observed: the complete card list is public, but the draft logs that supervised pick models train on do not yet exist. We study this day-zero regime directly. DraftFM is a discrete-choice policy that scores exactly the cards available in the current pack, conditioned on the drafted pool and the state of the draft. Every card enters as a frozen 775-dimensional function of its public card record, structured features and a fixed text embedding, with no card identities, set identities, or usage statistics anywhere in the model, so an unseen card is scored by the same machinery as a familiar one. A 1.6-million-parameter network fitted on 149 million human picks from 29 expansions predicts held-out picks in three expansions withheld in their entirety, reaching 50.8%, 60.4%, and 56.7% top-1 agreement, where uniform chance at the opening pick is about 7%. Refitted on all 32 observed expansions, the same architecture produced a card ranking for the then-unreleased set The Hobbit, sealed with its complete cryptographic provenance and published roughly 36 hours before the set became draftable on MTG Arena. The sealed ranking agrees with six independent expert reviewers roughly as much as those reviewers agree with one another. Evaluation against realized outcomes is committed to a follow-on note, whatever it shows.

</details>


### [179] [A Two-Stage Time-Aware Transformer for Short-Horizon AECOPD Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19578)
*Dongyang Wang,Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，直接处理家庭呼吸机最近七天的原始压力与流量波形数据，先通过分类模型识别AECOPD高风险患者，再通过回归模型预测事件发生前的剩余天数，为临床提供早期预警和可操作的提前量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD病情可快速恶化，及时预测是临床重点。现有机器学习方法依赖间断采集的临床变量，存在延迟，难以用于家庭监测场景；已有呼吸机方案要么将波形压缩为手工特征，要么仅做二分类风险预测，无法解决事件发生的时间节点问题。

Method: 构建两阶段框架：第一阶段分类模型识别高风险患者，第二阶段回归模型估计事件发生前剩余天数。模型直接基于家庭呼吸机最近七天的原始压力和流量波形数据进行训练与预测。

Result: 两阶段模型在风险分类和时间-事件估计上均优于传统基线模型；选定的第一阶段分类器F1=0.91，第二阶段回归模型RMSE=1.00天，R²=0.76。

Conclusion: 该两阶段方案能够在严重急性加重发生前为临床医生同时提供早期预警和可操作的提前量，提升家庭呼吸机数据的预测价值，弥补了既有方法在事件时间预测上的不足。

Abstract: Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) can worsen rapidly, making timely prediction a clinical priority. Most existing machine learning approaches rely on episodically collected clinical variables, introducing delays that limit their practical utility in home monitoring settings. Home ventilators offer a lower-latency alternative, producing a near-continuous record of respiratory status during daily use. However existing ventilator-based approaches either compress the waveform into handcrafted features or focus primarily on binary risk classification, leaving the timing of an impending event unresolved. In this paper, we present a two-stage framework that operates directly on raw pressure and flow waveforms from the most recent seven days of home ventilator use. The first-stage classification model identifies patients at high risk of a severe exacerbation. The second-stage regression model then estimates how many days remain before the event occurs. Our experimental results demonstrate that the two-stage model outperforms traditional baseline models on both risk classification and time-to-event estimation, with our selected Stage 1 classifier achieving F1 = 0.91 and our Stage 2 regression model achieving RMSE = 1.00 days and R^2 = 0.76, giving clinicians both an early warning and actionable lead time before a severe exacerbation occurs.

</details>


### [180] [Kähler landscapes for complex neural network descents and guarantees including a search and destroy of the Calabi-Yau manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19584)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息几何与复几何视角研究复杂参数化网络的损失景观。通过Wirtinger Hessian构造Kähler信息度量，并利用自然梯度下降在全纯切丛中演化。文章重点探讨Calabi-Yau信息流形上的损失景观，揭示了曲率（特别是Ricci曲率）与损失景观形态及神经网络收敛性保证之间的深层联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习中关于损失景观的分析多基于实值参数空间的欧几里得几何，难以充分捕捉复杂参数化网络（复参数网络）的结构特性。作者希望从信息论与复几何（特别是Kähler流形与Calabi-Yau流形）的视角，为复杂参数化网络的优化景观提供更本质的理论刻画，并理解曲率如何影响神经网络的训练动态与收敛保证。

Method: 本文采用几何-解析的方法论：1）在复参数空间上通过Wirtinger Hessian定义log-likelihood势，导出Kähler信息度量；2）采用自然梯度下降更新规则（损失梯度乘以逆度量），使下降轨迹保持在全纯切丛中；3）引入Calabi-Yau信息流形（非紧致情形），利用楔形无处消失的全纯形式作为Kähler形式的最高外积，建立常数行列式条件；4）在此框架下分析低秩度量与爆破解现象、负截曲率与负定Ricci曲率对损失景观的破坏效应；5）将几何结果与深度学习理论（初始化渐近、训练失败模式）建立联系。

Result: 论文揭示：在Calabi-Yau信息度量下，固定行列式条件可使度量近似低秩，进而导致优化过程中的爆破解出现；负截曲率（特别是负定Ricci曲率）会系统性恶化损失景观，破坏神经网络的收敛保证。同时，这些几何条件在神经网络初始化渐近与失败模式中均有对应表现。

Conclusion: 复杂参数化网络的损失景观可以由Kähler-CY型信息几何系统刻画，其曲率结构（尤其是负Ricci曲率）与神经网络的可训练性、收敛保证之间存在紧密的对应关系。这为从微分几何与复几何角度理解和改进深度网络优化提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study landscapes for complex-parameterized networks. Our approach is motivated with an information-theoretic manifold perspective of the parameter and via classical optimization guarantees although of complex geometric variety such as through Dolbeault asymptotics. The descent path admits a Kähler information metric under a cross-entropy via the Wirtinger Hessian on the log-likelihood potential. We restrict attention to a descent update rule with natural gradient descent via a differentiated loss scaled by the inverse metric, so the descent path remains in the holomorphic tangent bundle. We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. Under a Calabi-Yau metric, specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture, a wedged nowhere-vanishing holomorphic form is the top exterior product of the Kähler form up to constants, yielding a constant determinant condition.
