We introduce \method, a latent world-modeling framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization. Learned basis matrices analyze each target state into multiple components, and a dedicated prediction branch estimates each component from a shared context representation. Predictive regression preserves the factor magnitudes required for state synthesis, an orthogonality objective discourages repeated directions, factor-activity regularization maintains variation in projected targets, and online variance regularization discourages coordinate-wise encoder collapse. Predicted components are synthesized into a complete latent state that can be used by a readout, decoder, planner, or autoregressive rollout. The same predictive-state mechanism applies when the target is temporally future, spatially hidden, or another partial observation of the same system. Experiments on controlled vision, single-cell transcriptomics, longitudinal health records, continuous control, and molecular dynamics evaluate representation quality, forecasting, planning, and long-horizon stability.

</details>


### [215] [SAE-Xplainers: Rule-Based Feature Interpretation for Extreme Earth Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20117)
*Hugo Porta,Emanuele Dalsasso,Chang Xu,Theo Gnassounou,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于地理位置的稀疏自编码器（SAE）输入调制方法，并结合规则型SAE解释器集成，用于提升大规模天气与气候数据中极端事件深度学习模型的可解释性。在火灾预测、热带气旋和大气河检测三类任务上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天气与气候数据集为深度学习建模极端地球事件提供了新机遇，但其在实际业务中的应用受限于模型缺乏可解释性。虽然传统的稀疏自编码器（SAE）在文本和图像模态上能有效提取人类可理解的概念，但由于天气与气候数据的特殊性质（如空间分布、多模态特性），直接应用SAE分析极端事件仍具挑战。

Method: 本文提出两个核心组件：（i）基于地理位置的信息调制（geographic location-based modulation），对SAE的输入进行地理空间感知的调整，以捕获环境模式的局部语义含义；（ii）一个基于规则的SAE解释器集成（ensemble of rule-based SAE-Xplainers），用于解释从复杂的多模态环境预测因子中提取的高维特征，将抽象的深度学习特征展开为符合人类可理解性的规则。

Result: 在火灾预测、热带气旋检测和大气河检测三类极端事件任务上的实验表明：SAE输入调制方法同时提升了重构性能和特征利用率；所提出的SAE解释器集成能够将复杂的气候模式忠实地解释为与科学文献一致的人类可理解规则，并支持特征吸收（feature absorption）的识别。

Conclusion: 该方法在提升天气与气候预测模型可解释性的同时，不牺牲模型的表达能力，有助于弥合深度学习在极端事件预测领域从研究走向业务落地的鸿沟。通过将高维特征展开为科学文献一致的规则，为领域专家理解和验证模型行为提供了可操作的工具。

Abstract: The emergence of large-scale Weather and Climate (W&C) datasets offers new opportunities for modeling extreme Earth events (ExEE) and their impacts using deep learning. However, their adoption in operational settings remains limited by the lack of models' interpretability. While for conventional text and image modalities, tools such as Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have proven effective for extracting human-understandable concepts, their use for the analysis of ExEE remains challenging due to the nature of W&C data. To address this, we introduce (i) a geographic location-based modulation of the inputs of SAE to capture the local semantic meaning of environmental patterns, and (ii) an ensemble of rule-based SAE-Xplainers to interpret the resulting high-dimensional features derived from complex, multi-modal environmental predictors. We evaluate our method on three ExEE types: the prediction of fires, and the detection of tropical cyclones and atmospheric rivers. We show that SAE input modulation improves both reconstruction performance and feature utilization, and that our SAE-Xplainers enable faithful interpretation of complex climatic patterns by unfolding them into human-understandable rules that are consistent with the scientific literature, while also supporting the identification of feature absorption.

</details>


### [216] [Evaluating Neural Cartographic Relief Shading for Urban Environments: A Downtown Calgary Study Using High-Resolution DEM and DSM Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20149)
*Emmanuel Stefanakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在加拿大卡尔加里市中心的高密度城市环境中，利用高分辨率DEM和DSM数据，系统比较了单方向/多方向解析山体阴影法与基于神经网络训练的Eduard系统（最初为瑞士风格山地地形绘制设计）在建筑、桥梁、街道、树木等城市基础设施上的制图表现。研究发现Eduard通过参数调优（如地形类型、微/宏观泛化、平坦区域细节）虽不能完美再现城市形态，但在某些场景下比传统解析法更具视觉强度和制图实用性，同时也暴露出因训练数据偏向高山地形而导致的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络山体阴影系统（如Eduard）主要针对自然山地景观训练，其在高度人工化的城市环境中的表现尚未得到系统评估。作者希望通过探索性比较，回答两个核心问题：一是参数调优能否使面向山地训练的神经网络在城市地形中产出制图可用的结果；二是在哪些城市场景下传统解析法仍更可靠，哪些场景下神经方法具有意外优势。

Method: 采用探索性对比研究设计，在卡尔加里市中心使用高分辨率DEM和DSM数据，对比三种方法：单方向解析山体阴影、多方向解析山体阴影、以及Eduard神经网路生成的地形晕渲。神经实验中保持大尺度阴影风格不变，重点调节地形类型、微/宏观泛化和平坦区域细节参数，进行定性视觉比较与制图效用分析。

Result: 研究识别了各方法的适用边界：解析山体阴影在某些场景下仍然更可靠，Eduard在特定参数组合下能产生视觉上更强且制图上有用的结果，但在某些场景中因对阿尔卑斯地形的训练偏差而失败。

Conclusion: 神经山体阴影方法可通过参数调优部分适应城市环境，但现有训练数据的地形偏向限制了其全面适用性。作者主张未来应针对城市地表形态进行专门的模型训练和评估，而非仅依赖调参来弥合训练域与目标域之间的差距。

Abstract: This article explores the performance of analytical and neural-based hillshading methods in a dense urban environment using high-resolution digital elevation model (DEM) and digital surface model (DSM) data for downtown Calgary. The study compares single-direction and multi-direction analytical hillshading with relief shading generated in Eduard, a machine-learning system originally developed to emulate Swiss-style shaded relief trained primarily on mountainous landscapes. Because Eduard was not designed for buildings, bridges, streets, trees, and other urban infrastructures, the central question is not whether it perfectly reproduces urban morphology, but whether parameter tuning can nevertheless produce visually strong, cartographically useful, and in some cases superior results when compared with conventional analytical methods. The analysis focuses especially on terrain type, micro and macro generalization, and flat-area detail parameters, while keeping the large-scale shading style constant throughout the neural experiments. The article is structured as an exploratory comparison rather than a benchmark of universal best practice. It aims to identify where analytical hillshading remains more reliable, where Eduard offers unexpected strengths, and where neural shading fails because of its training bias toward alpine terrain. The study contributes to current work on terrain representation by testing whether a neural approach designed for natural landforms can be adapted to a highly built urban setting, and it concludes by arguing for future model training and evaluation specifically targeted at urban relief shading.

</details>


### [217] [Ask Self, Ask Others: Relation Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20172)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Relation，一种替代注意力机制的token混合范式：先将成对证据组织为显式的Self与Exchange关系，再从中推导信息流。在多个模型规模下，Full Relation优于标准多头注意力，并配套了FlashRelation、Linear Relation、Hybrid Relation及KV风格的关系缓存等高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的注意力机制直接从成对分数推导归一化信息流，但这种直接映射可能不是最优的token混合方式。作者认为在推导信息流之前，先显式地组织成对证据（分为Self与Exchange两类关系）可能带来更好的效果与效率。

Method: 提出Relation作为新的token混合原语，包含：(1) Full Relation——完整的显式关系组织；(2) FlashRelation——高效的融合版本；(3) Linear Relation——线性复杂度变体；(4) Hybrid Relation——混合75% Linear与Full Relation层；(5) KV-style Relation Cache——KV风格的关系缓存机制。

Result: 在约10M、30M和100M参数的匹配解码器模型上，Full Relation在所有三个规模下均获得比MHA更低的最终验证NLL；FlashRelation在固定上下文基准上比物化的Full Relation实现快3.60-4.41倍；在规模匹配的生产负载上达到PyTorch FlashAttention吞吐量的76.4-84.9%；Hybrid Relation以75% Linear层获得强劲的语言建模质量。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持"关系优先"的token混合观点：先询问Self，再询问Others，最后让信息流遵循关系（Flow Follow Relation）。Relation范式在模型质量和计算效率上均展现出有竞争力的表现。

Abstract: Attention directly derives normalized information flow from pairwise scores. We introduce Relation, an alternative token-mixing primitive that first organizes pairwise evidence into explicit Self and Exchange relations and derives information flow afterward. This relational organization gives rise to Full Relation, FlashRelation, Linear Relation, Hybrid Relation, and a KV-style Relation Cache. Across matched decoder-only models at approximately 10M, 30M, and 100M parameters, Full Relation achieves lower final validation NLL than MHA at all three scales. In a fixed-context reference benchmark, FlashRelation is 3.60-4.41x faster than the materialized Full Relation implementation. Across scale-matched production workloads, it reaches 76.4-84.9% of PyTorch FlashAttention throughput while executing the Full Relation operator. Hybrid Relation uses 75% Linear Relation layers and achieves strong language-modeling quality. These results support a relation-first view of token mixing: ask Self, ask Others, then let Flow follow Relation.

</details>


### [218] [A Standardized Framework for Machine Learning in Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20181)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro,Christian Bergler,Johann Jäger,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个标准化评估框架，将机器学习电力系统保护研究的评估设计规范化为七个必选维度，并通过PROTECT-90基准上的有界案例研究验证该框架，证明了评估假设的显式化有助于可比性、可审计性和未来认证导向的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习电力系统保护研究频繁报告接近完美的得分，但这些得分高度依赖评估设置；而保护任务、物理范围、测量方式、时序、目标、预处理和验证方式常常联合变化且说明不完整，导致结果难以比较、复现和审计。

Method: 提出包含七个必需研究维度的标准化框架：保护目标、物理范围、可观测性、时序与决策窗口、目标与样本有效性、验证协议、评估输出。将该框架实例化于公开的PROTECT-90电磁暂态基准（9022个模拟故障场景，90 kV双回线拓扑），开展有界案例研究，针对起始条件约束下的故障分类与定位任务，评估MLP在不同观测窗口（20 ms/50 ms）、不同可观测性水平下的表现，并与同步双端传统故障定位器进行对比。

Result: 在集中感知、20 ms窗口和按故障事件分组验证下，MLP的分类五折宏平均F1为0.991±0.001，定位平均绝对误差为线路长度的10.20±0.25%；扩展到50 ms决策窗口保持了任务依赖的性能不对称性；降低可观测性使MLP定位误差约翻倍但对分类影响不大；同步双端传统定位器在其更丰富的信息集下优于学习式定位器；测量退化测试表明干净条件下的预测性能不决定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架将评估假设转化为显式、可复现的证据，为机器学习保护功能的更可比、可审计评估以及未来认证导向评估提供了基础。

Abstract: Studies of machine-learning-based power-system protection increasingly report near-perfect scores, yet the meaning of those scores depends strongly on the evaluation setting. Protection task, physical scope, measurements, timing, targets, preprocessing, and validation often vary jointly and remain incompletely specified. This paper proposes a standardization-oriented framework that treats evaluation design as part of the scientific contribution. It defines seven required study dimensions: protection objective, physical scope, observability, timing and decision windows, targets and sample validity, validation protocol, and evaluation outputs. The framework is instantiated in a bounded case study on the public PROTECT-90 electromagnetic-transient benchmark, comprising 9022 simulated episodes from a 90 kV double-line topology, for onset-conditioned fault classification and localization. Under centralized sensing, simulation-metadata-aligned 20 ms windows, and episode-grouped validation, a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) achieved a five-fold mean macro-averaged F1 score of 0.991 +/- 0.001 for classification and a localization mean absolute error of 10.20 +/- 0.25% of line length (mean +/- std across episode-grouped folds). Extending the decision horizon to 50 ms preserved this task-dependent performance asymmetry, while reduced observability approximately doubled the MLP localization error but had little effect on classification. A synchronized two-ended conventional locator outperformed the learning locators under its richer clean information set, and measurement degradation showed that clean predictive performance did not determine robustness. The framework turns evaluation assumptions into explicit, reproducible evidence and provides a basis for more comparable, auditable evaluation and future certification-oriented assessment of machine-learning protection functions.

</details>


### [219] [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)
*Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,Elias Tsigaridas,Jules Tsukahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个确定性算法，能够精确计算任意二维模型的局部实对数规范阈值（RLCT），该算法适用于Kullback-Leibler散度与多项式接触等价的模型，并给出了复杂度上界，在多类模型（包括多项式神经网络）上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息准则（如BIC）在奇异模型（如深度学习）中因正则性假设不成立而失效。WBIC依赖局部学习系数λ（解析情形下等同于局部RLCT），但精确计算RLCT仅限于少数特例，目前只能使用基于采样的估计方法，缺乏确定性且精确的计算手段。

Method: 提出了第一个确定性算法，精确计算任意二维模型的局部RLCT。算法的适用条件是模型的Kullback-Leibler距离与某个多项式接触等价（contact equivalent）。文章推导了该算法的复杂度上界，并在广泛的模型类别中验证了其有效性。

Result: 算法在包括多项式神经网络在内的广泛模型类别中展示了有效性；与基于采样的估计方法相比，精确计算不仅能提供校准采样估计器的ground truth，还能揭示采样方法无法发现的代数结构，且在浅层（shallow）场景下速度更快。

Conclusion: 该工作为奇异模型的学习系数提供了首个通用的确定性精确计算方法，填补了该领域仅有采样估计的空白，并为二维模型的学习系数研究提供了新的代数视角和高效工具。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [220] [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258)
*MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出数据信息质心分裂（DICS）框架，利用基于数据驱动的先验信息（聚类和类感知结构）构建紧凑的候选分裂点集，在保持分类树预测性能的同时显著减少分裂搜索空间、降低训练时间，并提供了不劣于穷举搜索的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树模型因其可解释性和优秀的实证表现而被广泛使用，但训练过程中在每个节点进行的候选分裂点穷举搜索在大规模和高维数据集上计算成本极高，成为决策树训练的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出DICS（Data-Informed Centroid Splitting）框架，基于聚类方法利用数据驱动的先验信息（包括类别感知结构）构建一个紧凑且信息量高的候选分裂点集合，从而大幅缩减分裂搜索空间。该方法可无缝集成到分类树、随机森林和梯度提升模型中。

Result: 理论分析表明在给定假设下DICS不会降低分类树的性能；大量实验（涵盖合成数据集和基准数据集）证明DICS在保持与穷举搜索相当的分类精度的同时，显著缩短了训练时间，并适用于多种集成学习框架。

Conclusion: 将数据信息先验引入分裂点选择是提升可扩展分类树学习效率的有效途径，DICS在计算效率与预测性能之间取得了良好的平衡，为大规模决策树训练提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Decision tree-based models are widely used in machine learning due to their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, training decision trees can be computationally expensive, particularly for large and high-dimensional datasets, largely due to the exhaustive search over candidate splits at each node. To improve computational efficiency, we propose Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS), a clustering-based framework that constructs a compact and informative set of candidate splits using data-driven priors. By incorporating class-aware structure, DICS significantly reduces the split search space for classification tasks while preserving predictive performance. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search. DICS can be incorporated into classification trees, random forests, and gradient-boosting models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [221] [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Pranuthi Tenali,Arun Badi,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用PCMCI+算法从105份家庭睡眠呼吸暂停测试（HSAT）记录中学习睡眠呼吸障碍的动态因果图，通过分窗分数变量、边缘黑名单和自助聚合等方法，揭示了不同性别和年龄亚组之间因果结构的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果动力学复杂且因患者群体而异，这阻碍了针对性干预措施的开发。理解不同人群（性别、年龄）中因果结构的差异，有助于制定更精准的治疗策略。

Method: 使用PCMCI+算法对105份HSAT录制的分窗分数变量进行因果结构学习；利用领域知识进行边缘黑名单约束；采用自助聚合（bootstrap aggregation）来处理小亚组样本量问题。

Result: 学习到的因果图显示，时间自依赖和呼吸暂停-去饱和（apnea-desaturation）关系在所有亚组中持续存在，而其他因果关系在不同性别和年龄亚组之间差异显著。

Conclusion: 动态因果图学习可以揭示睡眠呼吸障碍在不同人群中的因果结构差异，其中部分因果机制具有群体普遍性，而其他机制则呈现出人群特异性，这为针对特定亚组的干预设计提供了依据。

Abstract: The causal dynamics of sleep-disordered breathing are complex and vary across patient populations, hindering the development of targeted interventions. We learn dynamic causal graphs of sleep-disordered breathing from Home Sleep Apnea Test (HSAT) recordings, revealing systematic differences in causal structure across sex and age subcohorts. We do so using the PCMCI+ algorithm on windowed fractional variables derived from 105 HSAT recordings, exploiting domain knowledge via edge blacklisting and employing bootstrap aggregation to address small subcohort sizes. The learned graphs show that temporal self-dependencies and the apnea-desaturation relationship persist across all cohorts, while other relationships vary substantially.

</details>


### [222] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分辨率感知的物理支持推断方法，联合考虑了学得字典和部署信号表示中的不确定性，用于在高度相干的字典下获得物理上有意义的原子支持推断，并引入了自适应有限基过程AEB以避免过精确的物理推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在字典学习后进行稀疏追踪，即使其物理解释不受校准数据支持，也可能产生精确的原子支持。尤其在高度相干的字典中，不同的校准兼容字典可能对相同选定支持赋予不同的物理意义，因此需要一种能联合考虑字典和信号表示不确定性的物理支持推断方法。

Method: 提出交叉字典置信对应方法，保留校准兼容的字典和部署兼容的稀疏表示，将幸存解释投影到物理支持空间。引入主动端点括定（AEB）自适应有限基过程，只评估仍可能影响物理报告的候选方案，否则安全地粗化或弃权。建立了最小最大物理分辨率的理论界限δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s,1/(√N s²)}。

Result: 当部署数据解析相干块解释及其原子支持时，N个校准信号的最小最大物理分辨率满足δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s,1/(√N s²)}，相对分辨率由方向信息尺度Ns^6控制。有限基实验（包括四区域合成应用）显示，点值插值选择器可能产生物理上的过度精确结果，而AEB以更少的候选评估避免了不支持的细化。

Conclusion: 部署复制只有在方向变化无法通过调整活动系数吸收时才改善物理定位。AEB能够在计算效率与物理推断精度之间取得平衡，避免在不支持的细化上进行不必要的计算投入。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [223] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BERT-LER，一种基于BERT架构的EHR基础模型，利用百分位分箱将实验室检验结果编码为离散token保留分级信息，并结合Integrated Gradients实现token级可解释性。在EHRShot基准套件和哮喘进展研究中，该模型在预测性能上与公开基准竞争，在实验室相关任务上常超越，且归因结果与临床已知风险因素一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化EHR预测模型很少同时强调定量实验室信息的编码和针对输入医疗事件的可解释性；传统方法要么只处理编码化事件而忽视实验室检验的量化信息，要么在可解释性上缺乏对输入序列的细粒度归因。作者旨在弥合这一方法学空白，在同一框架中统一实验室数值表征与模型可解释性，并验证其在标准临床预测任务之外能否泛化。

Method: 提出BERT-LER模型：采用BERT风格架构，将实验室检验结果通过百分位分箱编码为离散token以保留分级（graded）定量信息；在包含7500万患者的去标识化EHR数据集上进行预训练和微调；使用Integrated Gradients方法对输入EHR序列中的token进行归因，提供细粒度可解释性。评估使用公开EHRShot基准套件以及基于真实世界数据的哮喘进展严重程度研究。

Result: 在EHRShot和哮喘任务上，BERT-LER的预测性能与公开可用的基准模型相当，在实验室相关任务上通常超越这些基准；其归因结果与临床上已知的风险因素相符，验证了可解释性的可靠性。

Conclusion: BERT-LER展示了语言模型在结构化EHR上同时实现高性能预测与可解释性的可行性，其架构和可解释方法可推广到多种治疗领域和预测任务，填补了EHR基础模型在实验室数值表示与训练级归因方面的空白。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [224] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对天花板安装的FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi传感三种射频技术进行了受控对比，利用20名参与者在6种房间布局下的同步记录，在同一CNN架构下评估了细粒度10类人体活动识别和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务。结果显示IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别中表现最佳（89.0%宏F1），FMCW在未见房间布局下泛化最优（83.8%宏F1），睡眠监测任务所有技术均超过92%宏F1。研究揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同的射频技术（FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi感知）在基于射频的无接触医疗监测中日益重要，但现有研究在硬件、数据集和评估方法上各不相同，导致这些技术在相同部署条件下缺乏可比性。此外，天花板安装式雷达在医疗环境中具有实际部署和成本优势，但其性能尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 设计受控对比实验：使用20名参与者在6种房间布局下的同步记录数据，对天花板安装的FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi三种射频技术进行统一评估。所有技术均使用相同的卷积神经网络（CNN），分别执行细粒度10类人体活动识别（HAR）任务和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务。

Result: IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别中取得最高性能（89.0%宏F1）；FMCW在未见房间布局下泛化能力最佳（83.8%宏F1）；睡眠监测任务中，所有技术在未见环境下均超过92%宏F1。这些差异可由距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留能力来解释。

Conclusion: 研究发现识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间存在根本性权衡，该权衡可通过不同技术在距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留方面的差异来解释，为面向医疗健康的射频传感系统设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Incident-Data Robustness Analysis of the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications (2026): How a Community-Expert Ranking Holds Up Against a Large-Scale LLM Incident Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19266)
*Kyriakos "Rock" Lambros,Steve Wilson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对比了OWASP Top 10 LLM应用安全专家排名与实际安全事件数据排名的一致性。研究者构建了包含7,714个快照和6,639个标注样本的大规模LLM安全事件语料库，使用贝叶斯测量误差模型纠正分类器偏差，发现专家排名与数据排名的一致性极弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20），但专家排名仍具有稳健性。预注册的四类前沿分类器对比测试中无胜者，均未超越事件发生率的基线平衡精度0.863。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWASP Top 10是安全专家社区基于共识对LLM应用风险的重要性排序，但其是否与真实安全事件的数据记录相符尚不明确。作者希望检验专家共识是否与实证数据一致，从而为风险优先级提供更可靠的依据。

Method: 从CVE、GHSA、OSV和AIAAIC四个来源构建大规模LLM安全事件语料库（7,714个快照，6,639个标注，对应20项分类体系）；使用贝叶斯测量误差模型校准各分类的计数以纠正分类器的精确率和召回率偏差；以0.75专家权重与0.25数据权重的固定比例混合生成2026候选列表；通过Cohen's κ评估两个排名的相关性；预注册四类前沿分类器的bake-off对比测试。

Result: 专家排名与数据排名的一致性很弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20，90%置信区间跨越零）；四类前沿分类器在预注册测试中均无胜者，没有分类器超越事件发生率基线的平衡精度0.863；基线的排序经地面真值检验仍然成立（Spearman ρ=0.918）。

Conclusion: 尽管专家共识与实际事件数据之间的相关性很弱，但专家排名仍具有稳健性。数据驱动的排名可以作为辅助信号修正专家共识（25%权重），而不应推翻它。该分析属于探索性研究，不代表官方OWASP发布。

Abstract: The OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications ranks the risks that a community of security practitioners judges most important. We ask a narrower question: checked against the record of real incidents, does that expert ranking agree with the data? We assembled a large-scale corpus of LLM-security incidents (7,714 snapshotted and 6,639 labeled against the 20-entry taxonomy) drawn from CVE, GHSA, OSV, and AIAAIC, and derived an incident-based ranking with a Bayesian measurement-error model that corrects each category's count for classifier precision and recall. The 2026 candidate list blends the two signals at fixed weights, 0.75 on the expert vote and 0.25 on the data, so the corpus corrects the consensus without overturning it. The agreement between the two rankings is weak: Cohen's $κ\approx 0.20$, with a 90% interval that crosses zero. The expert ranking is nonetheless robust. A pre-registered bake-off of four frontier classifiers returns no winner. None beats the incidence floor's balanced accuracy of 0.863. A ground-truth check leaves the floor's ordering (Spearman $ρ= 0.918$ against held-out truth) in place. This is an exploratory analysis by two working-group members, not the official OWASP release, and it does not supersede the official list or process.

</details>


### [226] [ABEAT: Efficient and Anonymous Encryption for ABE-based Dynamic Group Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19302)
*Hongmiao Yu,Silas Richelson,Jiachen Chen,K. K. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABEAT，一个基于属性基加密（ABE）的高效匿名动态群组通信系统。ABEAT通过新的匿名KP-ABE方案隐藏密文中的明文属性以保持接收者匿名性，将非目标接收者的解密开销较现有技术降低90倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态群组通信需要保密性、灵活性和高效的多对多通信能力。虽然已有的ABE框架支持动态群组形成与变更，但缺乏对消息接收者匿名性的保护。在应急响应等场景中，接收者匿名性至关重要，因此需要在不显著增加开销的前提下实现匿名性的群组通信系统。

Method: ABEAT基于图命名空间和KP-ABE构建框架，核心创新是提出新的匿名KP-ABE方案：在密文中隐藏明文属性以维持接收者匿名，同时设计机制防止多种试图破坏匿名性的攻击。系统提供快速的接收者验证机制，使非目标接收者能快速判定自己是否为目标，从而大幅降低非接收者的解密开销。

Result: ABEAT将非目标接收者的解密开销比当前最优的隐藏向量加密（HVE）方法降低超过90倍，且比不提供匿名性的FABEO方案还少40%的开销。

Conclusion: ABEAT实现了在动态群组通信中同时兼顾高效性和接收者匿名性的目标，在性能上显著优于现有方案，为需要匿名保障的场景（如应急响应）提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential communication among a dynamic group of participants that ensures flexible and efficient many-to- many communication is highly desired capability. We leverage attribute-based encryption (ABE) for confidential group communication and enhance it by a graph-based namespace to create an efficient framework that allows groups to be formed and changed dynamically. In this paper, we focus on the important additional need to maintain the anonymity of recipients of a message, when using ABE for group communication for a variety of usage scenarios (e.g., emergency response).
  We propose ABEAT, an efficient and anonymous dynamic group communication system that also minimizes overhead on receivers who are not the intended recipients of a message. In ABEAT, we propose a new anonymous KP-ABE approach to maintain recipient anonymity. ABEAT hides the clear attribute in the ciphertext of KP-ABE, and also prevents several attacks that seek to break anonymity. ABEAT provides fast recipient verification, dramatically lowering the decryption overhead for non-recipients by more than a factor of 90 versus the current state of the art such as hidden vector encryption (HVE). In fact, it is even 40% less than FABEO, which offers no anonymity.

</details>


### [227] [Aray: Deterministic-First Synthesis of Benign Artifacts for YARA Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19387)
*Emanuel C. A. Valente,Lourenço A. P. Júnior,Leonardo Gonçalves Chahud,Júlio Cezar Estrella,Marcus Botacin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Aray，一个确定性优先的YARA规则解释器和正向测试样本合成器。它能在不复制原始恶意软件行为的前提下，为YARA规则生成匹配的正向测试文件（fixture）。在416条公开规则条目上，406条（97.6%）成功生成并通过原始规则验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YARA规则易于分发，但用于展示正向匹配的恶意软件样本本身难以分发，给存储、持续集成、灾难恢复演练和可复现的扫描器验证带来困难。构造替代测试文件不仅仅是嵌入字面量，因为YARA条件可组合多种匹配逻辑（备选、计数、偏移、整数读取、可执行容器约束等），且生成的文件不应再现恶意行为。

Method: Aray采用确定性优先的架构：模型可提出构造性归一化或类型化提取回退方案，但绝不使用后端源代码或二进制结构。由传统代码验证归一化后的规则、推导字符串和整数见证（witness），并执行提取、路由、冲突检查布局以及ELF、PE或通用序列化。仅将残留的归一化语义交给有界模型判定器（bounded model judge）。

Result: 在416条公开规则条目上评估：182条无需模型辅助即可归一化，234条经模型归一化后通过。可构造性预检接纳406条规则，所有被接纳的fixture均匹配其对应的上游原始规则。整体成功率406/416（97.6%），可构造规则内406/406，另有10条预期预检拒绝。实现的不可达端点确认生成期间模型调用为零次。

Conclusion: Aray证明了仅用传统解释器加有限模型辅助即可为绝大多数YARA规则合成有效的正向测试文件。原始的规则oracle用于验证生成fixture对源规则的匹配；证明对所有可能文件的蕴含关系是另一个更强的独立目标。两个锚定正则修复在最终运行前完成，因此结果是修复后系统的数据而非留出估计。

Abstract: A YARA rule is easy to distribute, but the malware sample used to demonstrate a positive match is not. This complicates storage, continuous integration, disaster-recovery exercises, and reproducible scanner validation. Constructing a replacement fixture requires more than embedding literals: YARA conditions can combine alternatives, counts, offsets, integer reads, and executable-container constraints, while the resulting file should not reproduce malware behavior. Positive validation is existential: it requires one file-level member of a rule's match set, not reconstruction of the originating sample. We present Aray, a deterministic-first YARA interpreter and positive-fixture synthesizer. Models may propose constructive normalizations or typed extraction fallbacks, but never backend source or binary structure. Conventional code validates normalized rules, derives string and integer witnesses, and performs extraction, routing, collision-checked layout, and ELF, PE, or generic serialization. Only residual normalization semantics reach a bounded model judge. We evaluated Aray over 416 public-rule entries. Normalization accepted 182 entries without model assistance and 234 after model normalization. Constructibility preflight admitted 406 entries, and every admitted fixture matched its upstream original rule. This yields 406/416 (97.6%) overall and 406/406 among constructible rules, with ten expected preflight dispositions and no scanner mismatches or construction failures. An unreachable endpoint confirmed zero model invocations during realization. The original-rule oracle validates generated fixtures against their source rules; proving implication for all possible files is a separate, stronger objective. Two anchored-regex failures were repaired before the final run, so these are post-fix systems results, not a held-out estimate.

</details>


### [228] [Redactable blockchains and polynomial equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19401)
*Alexander Demin,Alexey Ovchinnikov,Vladimir Shpilrain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文开发了新的工具，用于构建具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构（redactable authenticated data structures）。其核心创新在于：所提出的单向函数求逆等价于求解多变量多项式方程组，这被认为是量子安全的（目前没有已知的高效量子算法可以解决该问题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算的发展，传统基于数论难题（如大整数分解、离散对数）的密码学方案面临威胁。可编辑认证数据结构需要在允许数据被编辑（redaction）的同时保持认证的完整性，而现有方案在量子计算时代缺乏安全性保障。因此需要构建后量子安全的方案。

Method: 提出一种新的单向函数构造方法，其安全性基于求解多变量多项式方程组（MPE，Multivariate Polynomial Equation）的困难性。在构造可编辑认证数据结构时，将数据编辑操作的安全性归结为对单向函数求逆的难度——即求解超过一个变量的多项式方程组。

Result: 成功构建了具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构，其安全性建立在多变量多项式方程求解问题的困难性之上，这是当前被认为是量子安全（quantum-safe）的假设。

Conclusion: 所提出的构造方案在合理选择参数的前提下，能够抵御已知的量子攻击，为可编辑认证数据结构提供了后量子安全的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop new tools for constructing redactable authenticated data structures with post-quantum security. In our construction, inverting the proposed one-way function means solving a polynomial equation (or a system of polynomial equations) in more than one variable. This is presently considered quantum-safe, i.e., there is no known quantum algorithm that could solve this problem efficiently if parameters are chosen wisely.

</details>


### [229] [A Federated Learning Framework for Privacy-Preserving Oral Cancer Screening on Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19462)
*Lena D. Swamikannan,Akshay Bhagwan Sonawane,Jay S. Patel,C. S. Mani,Lakshmi Narayana,Lakshman Tamil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个实用的联邦学习框架，支持跨地理分布的医学AI研究者协作开发口腔癌筛查模型。通过Tailscale提供安全网络，利用Flower框架实现服务端聚合，并评估了多种轻量级移动端架构，其中基于MobileNetV4-Conv-S的全局模型在FedAvg聚合下取得最优性能（AUC 0.929，准确率87%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学领域（尤其口腔临床影像）数据获取受限于法规和患者隐私，难以集中训练稳健的AI模型。联邦学习可在不共享患者数据的前提下实现跨机构协作建模，因此需要构建一个实用的FL框架来支持口腔癌筛查模型的分布式开发。

Method: 采用实用FL框架：客户端通过Tailscale互联提供安全网络与实时通信；使用Flower框架进行服务端聚合，客户端手动部署编排（不使用企业级FL平台）；针对智能手机端筛查应用，评估MobileNetV2、MobileNetV3Large和MobileNetV4-Conv-Small等轻量级移动端架构，并用FedAvg聚合全局模型。

Result: 在FedAvg聚合的全局轻量级模型中，基于MNv4-Conv-S的全局模型（GM-V4）表现最佳，达到AUC 0.929和准确率87%。

Conclusion: 所提出的联邦学习框架能够有效支持跨地域医学AI协作，且MobileNetV4-Conv-Small在口腔癌筛查场景中展现出最佳的精度与移动端适配综合表现。

Abstract: Data are the cornerstone of robust AI models. However, in the medical domain, access to reliable data is constrained by regulatory requirements and patient privacy, and clinical oral images are particularly difficult to obtain. Federated learning (FL) mitigates these constraints by enabling collaborative model development across decentralized datasets without centralizing or sharing patient data.
  This work presents a practical FL framework that supports geographically distributed collaboration among AI healthcare researchers and facilitates the development of robust models for oral cancer screening. Client devices were interconnected via Tailscale to provide secure networking and real-time communication. We implemented the FL workflow using the Flower framework for server-side aggregation, while client deployment and orchestration were configured manually; no enterprise FL platforms were used. To support a smartphone-based screening application, we evaluated lightweight, mobile-friendly architectures including MobileNetV2, MobileNetV3Large, and MobileNetV4-Conv-Small (MNv4-Conv-S). Across the global lightweight models aggregated using FedAvg, the MNv4-Conv-S based global model (GM-V4) achieved the best performance, reaching an AUC of 0.929 and an accuracy of 87%

</details>


### [230] [AEGIS: Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield - Triple-Channel Gradient Masking for Privacy-Preserving Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19534)
*Ye Tao,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Xin Wang,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS（注意力-嵌入梯度隔离盾），一种轻量级防御方法，通过三个反向路径操作同时封堵联邦学习中Transformer梯度的三条令牌泄露通道（注意力输出投影梯度、嵌入梯度、MLP扩展梯度），在不改变架构的前提下将令牌恢复率降至接近零，同时保持或提升模型效用，并提供形式化保证和自适应对抗验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度反演攻击能从联邦学习中共享的梯度恢复私有训练文本，构成严重威胁。现有防御最多只处理一条泄露通道，要么降低模型效用，要么留下其余结构信号，无法提供全面防护。

Method: AEGIS通过三项反向路径操作防御三条泄露通道：1) 冻结注意力投影参数，从构造上消除通道1（低秩子空间编码输入嵌入）；2) 向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声，破坏通道2的令牌存在信号（行范数稀疏性）；3) 向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块噪声，遮蔽通道3。掩蔽后的同一梯度同时驱动本地优化器和服务器导出，确保两侧均无干净信号。

Result: 在11个模型和6个数据集上，AEGIS将针对各类梯度反演攻击（分析型与优化型）的令牌恢复率降至接近零，同时保持或提升模型效用。对通道1和2提供形式化保证，并在完全了解机制的适应性攻击者下验证了完整防御。

Conclusion: AEGIS是首个同时封堵全部三条Transformer梯度令牌泄露通道的轻量级防御方法，无需架构改动即可实现近似零令牌泄露，具有理论保证并兼顾模型效用。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks recover private training text from gradients shared in federated learning, posing a serious threat to collaborative model training. Through our analysis of transformer gradient structure, we identify three channels through which private token information leaks: the attention output projection gradient exposes a low-rank subspace that encodes input embeddings (Channel 1), the embedding gradient's row-norm sparsity directly reveals which tokens are present (Channel 2), and the MLP expansion gradient carries a recoverable subspace signal analogous to Channel 1 (Channel 3). State-of-the-art attacks exploit these channels analytically to achieve near-exact token recovery in seconds. Existing defences address at most one channel and either degrade model utility or leave the remaining structural signals intact.
  We introduce AEGIS (Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield), a lightweight defence that closes all three analytical channels with three backward-path operations requiring no architectural changes: freezing attention projection parameters eliminates Channel 1 by construction, calibrated noise injection into the embedding gradient destroys Channel 2's token-presence signal, and analogous per-block noise injection into the MLP expansion gradient masks Channel 3. The same masked gradient drives both the local optimiser step and the server export, so no clean signal is retained on either side.
  Evaluated across 11 models and six datasets, AEGIS reduces token recovery rates to near zero against a range of gradient inversion attacks, both analytical and optimisation-based, while preserving or improving model utility. We provide formal guarantees for Channels 1 and 2 and validate the full defence empirically against adaptive adversaries with complete knowledge of the mechanism.

</details>


### [231] [Enhancing Privacy in Federated Learning via Dual Obfuscation of Gradients and Training Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19650)
*Yuki Itabashi,Hiroto Sawada,Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种双重混淆方法，结合随机二元权重梯度稀疏化与图像加密技术，以增强联邦学习对图像重建攻击（如APRIL）的防御能力，实验表明在ViT图像分类任务上有效减少视觉信息泄露且不引入额外分类性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许在数据本地化的情况下协作训练模型，但已有研究表明训练数据可从共享的模型更新中被重建。现有的单一防御手段（如梯度修改或图像加密）均存在不足，需要一种结合多种互补机制的防御方法来增强对图像重建攻击的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出双重混淆方法，包含两个互补组件：1）基于随机二元权重的鲁棒性增强技术，将部分梯度元素随机置零以减少攻击者可用的原始梯度信息量；2）图像加密技术，降低重建图像的视觉可解释性。图像加密支持每个客户端和每张图像使用独立密钥，避免显式密钥共享。

Result: 在基于Vision Transformer（ViT）的图像分类任务上，该方法在评估设置下减少了Attention Privacy Leakage（APRIL）攻击恢复的视觉信息，且未导致超越图像加密本身所引入的分类性能额外退化。

Conclusion: 虽然组合方法不提供绝对的安全保证，但实验结果证明了在隐私增强联邦学习中结合梯度修改与图像加密的潜在优势，为未来联邦学习隐私保护提供了新方向。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training while keeping data locally at each client; however, recent studies have shown that training data can be reconstructed from shared model updates. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual obfuscation method that enhances robustness against image restoration attacks by jointly obfuscating updated information and training images. The proposed method combines a robustness enhancement technique based on random binary weights, which randomly sets a portion of gradient elements to zero, with an image encryption technique. These techniques provide complementary protection by reducing the amount of original gradient information available to an attacker and the visual interpretability of reconstructed images, respectively. Furthermore, the image encryption technique allows independent keys to be used for each client and each image, avoiding explicit key sharing. Experimental results on an image classification task using a Vision Transformer (ViT) show that the proposed method reduces the visual information recovered by Attention Privacy Leakage (APRIL) under the evaluated settings without causing additional degradation in classification performance beyond that caused by image encryption. Although the proposed combination does not provide an absolute security guarantee, the results demonstrate the potential benefit of combining gradient modification and image encryption for privacy-enhanced federated learning.

</details>


### [232] [TrustRAG: Blockchain-Enhanced RAG via Committee-Based Credibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20097)
*Baixiang Liu,Haotian Che,Yuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustRAG是一个基于委员会和区块链的检索增强生成系统，通过零知识证明、安全多方计算和跨链哈希承诺来确保检索文档的可验证性、完整性和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统依赖集中的、缺乏监督的数据库，无法验证文档来源、检测篡改或评估可信度，在医疗、金融、物流和法律等对信息时效性和准确性要求极高的领域，错误的或被操纵的文档会导致严重决策失误。

Method: TrustRAG采用委员会机制：文档在使用前由领域专家委员会通过零知识协议进行认证，委员会的隐藏评分通过安全多方计算（MPC）合成为可验证的信任分数；文档数据和评分通过跨链哈希承诺进行联合维护，确保任何文档或评分无法被静默修改或删除，排名可独立重放和验证。

Result: 论文提出了一种可验证的RAG框架，实现了文档认证、信任评分、防篡改存储和排名可审计性，但摘要中未提供具体实验定量结果。

Conclusion: TrustRAG通过结合密码学协议（零知识证明、MPC）与区块链技术，解决了RAG系统中文档来源可验证性、完整性和信任评估的核心问题，为高可靠性领域的RAG应用提供了安全保障方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets Large Language Models (LLMs) pull in up-to-date, domain-specific information instead of relying only on what they were trained on. Yet most RAG systems still draw from centralized databases with limited oversight, making it difficult to verify where a document came from, whether it has been tampered with, or whether it should be trusted at all. This is a serious problem in domains where both the timeliness and accuracy of retrieved content are critical, such as healthcare, finance, logistics, and legal case law, where a wrong or manipulated document can directly lead to bad decisions.
  We present TrustRAG, a committee-based, blockchain-backed RAG system: before a document is used, it is certified by a committee of domain experts through a zero-knowledge protocol, and the committee's hidden scores are combined via secure multi-party computation into a trust score that any client can verify. These scores, along with the underlying document data, are maintained jointly across chains through hash commitments, so no document or score can be silently altered or dropped, and every ranking can be independently replayed and checked.

</details>


### [233] [TGL-APT: Temporal Graph Learning with Graph Distillation for Efficient APT Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19750)
*Jing Chen,Ayong Ye,Yuanhuang Liu,Yuexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TGL-APT是一个基于信息瓶颈节点引导的自适应APT入侵调查框架，通过图蒸馏、自适应时序图学习和跨时空指纹对齐三个组件，在DARPA E3数据集上实现88.9%-95.7%的F1分数，同时相比KAIROS降低约39%训练时间、33%检测延迟和22%内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击具有隐蔽性和多阶段特性，传统检测方法难以有效应对。虽然溯源图（provenance graph）能为攻击调查提供丰富的行为上下文，但攻击相关证据稀疏且嵌入大量常规系统活动中，导致全图学习计算开销大且难以在长攻击序列中进行关联。

Method: TGL-APT基于"攻击相关信息非均匀分布且常由结构上有影响力或行为上独特的实体（信息瓶颈节点）介导"的观察，提出三个互补组件：(1)信息瓶颈引导的图蒸馏，抑制溯源冗余并保持结构失真有界和因果可达性；(2)自适应时序图学习，根据节点相关性演化持续精炼核心节点集；(3)跨时空攻击指纹对齐，关联不同实体和时间窗口中的分散可疑活动。最终通过因果扩展和阶段表征重构完整攻击过程。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上分别取得95.7%、90.9%和88.9%的F1分数，相比KAIROS，训练时间减少约39%，检测延迟降低约33%，内存使用减少约22%。

Conclusion: TGL-APT有效平衡了检测性能、计算效率和调查能力，为基于溯源图的APT分析提供了一种高效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) attacks pose a critical challenge to modern systems, as their stealthy, multi-stage nature renders conventional detection methods ineffective. While provenance graphs provide rich behavioral context for attack investigation, attack-relevant evidence is often sparse and embedded in large volumes of routine system activity, making full-graph learning both computationally expensive and difficult to correlate over long attack sequences. We present TGL-APT, an adaptive investigation framework built on the observation that attack-relevant information is non-uniformly distributed and often mediated by structurally influential or behaviorally distinctive entities, which we characterize as information-bottleneck nodes. TGL-APT combines three complementary components: (1) information-bottleneck-guided graph distillation that suppresses provenance redundancy while bounding structural distortion and preserving causal reachability; (2) adaptive temporal graph learning that continuously refines the core node set as node relevance evolves; and (3) cross-spatiotemporal attack fingerprint alignment that associates fragmented suspicious activities across different entities and time windows. Finally, causal expansion and stage characterization reconstruct coherent attack processes for investigation. Experiments on three DARPA E3 datasets show F1-scores of 95.7%, 90.9%, and 88.9%, while reducing training time, detection latency, and memory usage by approximately 39%, 33%, and 22%, respectively, compared with KAIROS. These results demonstrate that TGL-APT effectively balances detection performance, computational efficiency, and investigation capability for provenance-based APT analysis.

</details>


### [234] [Survival of~the~Stealthiest: Evolving Low-Entropy Ransomware via~Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19821)
*Efrat Levenberg,Kristina Sviazhina,Ayelet Butman,Pierre Parrend,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将勒索软件执行建模为基于搜索的软件工程（SBSE）优化问题，利用遗传算法（GA）在系统活动统计偏差的硬约束下优化数据加密过程，从而生成能规避行为监控和指纹识别技术的隐蔽攻击模式，展示了搜索方法在生成规避性恶意软件方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统勒索软件依赖大规模加密过程，容易被现代防御系统立即检测。现代威胁呈现出"持久性差距"——攻击希望保持隐蔽数小时而非数分钟，因此需要一种新的攻击范式来实现长期隐蔽。

Method: 将勒索软件执行重新定义为SBSE优化问题，使用遗传算法（GA）在系统活动统计偏差的硬性约束下优化数据加密行为，目标是最大化加密效果的同时最小化与正常系统行为的偏离度。

Result: 实验证明进化出的攻击模式能够规避基于指纹技术的行为监控系统，验证了搜索方法在生成规避性恶意软件方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 搜索驱动的方法为生成隐蔽性强、难以检测的恶意软件提供了强大框架，这对自动化软件防御构成了新的挑战，凸显了下一代防御系统需要应对的威胁形态。

Abstract: Traditional ransomware deployment often relies on massive encryption procedure, triggering immediate detection by modern defense systems. This work introduces a paradigm shift in cryptographic attacks by framing ransomware execution as a Search-Based Software Engineering (SBSE) optimization problem. This approach addresses the persistence gap observed in modern threats, where attacks aim to remain undercover for hours rather than minutes. Using a Genetic Algorithm (GA), we optimize data encryption under a hard constraint on the statistical deviation from baseline system activity. We demonstrate that our evolved attack patterns can evade behavioral monitors under fingerprinting techniques. Our results suggest that search-based methods provide a powerful framework for generating evasive malware, highlighting an emerging challenge for automated software defense.

</details>


### [235] [MaliciousSkillBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19901)
*Yue Wang,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Ying Zhang,Yuekang Li,Zhenyu Chen,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MaliciousSkillBench，一个综合性的恶意Agent Skill检测基准。通过整合13个公开来源（其中11个贡献核心恶意样本），将8,414条原始恶意记录规约为7,539个归一化唯一身份（属于4,588个操作结构家族）。在保守的交叉标签冲突排除后，基准包含9,740个Skills（7,505恶意+2,235良性）。评估发现，现有学习型检测器在Source-Disjoint评估下性能显著下降（F1从0.882-0.932降至0.653-0.665），词TF-IDF SVM表现最强但良性误报率高达62.4%，现成扫描器在不牺牲恶意召回率的情况下无法有效降低误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills扩展了LLM智能体的能力，以可复用指令包形式存在（可能包含脚本、资源和服务配置），这为恶意行为的直接分发创造了通道。然而现有的恶意Skill数据集在来源、工件格式、证据体系和良性覆盖上碎片化严重，且存在重复和结构相关的内容，使得直接聚合和评估变得困难。需要一个统一的基准来衡量恶意Skill检测方法的真实性能。

Method: 1) 数据整合：从13个公开来源整合数据，11个贡献核心恶意工件，将8,414条原始恶意记录通过归一化去重为7,539个唯一身份，聚类为4,588个操作结构家族；2) 基准构建：保守地排除交叉标签冲突后，构建包含9,740个Skills的基准（7,505恶意+2,235良性）；3) 攻击分类协调：将4,983个恶意身份映射到统一的11个攻击类别；4) 系统评估：评测3个学习型文本检测器和3个现成Skill扫描器，采用Random、structural-disjoint和Source-Disjoint三种评估设置。

Result: 学习型检测器在Random评估下达到0.882-0.932的Random Macro-F1，但在Source-Disjoint评估下降至0.653-0.665。最强的词TF-IDF SVM在Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint上分别得分0.932/0.916/0.665，保持95.6%恶意召回但产生62.4%的良性误报率。现成扫描器在各类来源中表现不均，倾向于牺牲恶意召回来降低误报。研究还发现各来源的威胁组成存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 可靠的恶意Skill检测需要更广泛的跨源基准覆盖，以及同时衡量攻击检测能力和良性过度标记的联合评估。现有方法在分布外Source-Disjoint场景下表现不佳，且都存在召回率与误报率之间的权衡问题，表明该领域仍需进一步研究以构建泛化性强、误报率低的恶意Skill检测系统。

Abstract: Agent Skills extend LLM agents with reusable instruction packages that may also include scripts, resources, and service configuration. This creates a direct distribution channel for malicious behavior, yet existing malicious-Skill datasets are fragmented across sources, artifact formats, evidence regimes, and benign coverage; duplicated and structurally related content further complicates direct aggregation and evaluation. We present MaliciousSkillBench, a comprehensive benchmark for malicious Agent Skill detection. We consolidate 13 public sources, 11 of which contribute Core malicious artifacts, and reduce 8,414 raw malicious records to 7,539 normalized-unique identities in 4,588 operational structural families. After conservative cross-label conflict exclusion, the primary benchmark contains 9,740 Skills: 7,505 malicious and 2,235 benign. To characterize its coverage, we harmonize 11 attack categories for 4,983 malicious identities with supported source-native mappings and find substantial differences in threat composition across sources. We then evaluate three learned text detectors and three off-the-shelf Skill scanners. Learned detectors achieve 0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1 but only 0.653-0.665 under Source-Disjoint evaluation; the strongest word TF-IDF SVM scores 0.932/0.916/0.665 on Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint while retaining 95.6% malicious recall but producing 62.4% benign FPR on held-out sources. Off-the-shelf scanners occupy different but also unsatisfactory operating regimes, reducing false positives only at the cost of sharply lower malicious recall. Together, these results show that reliable malicious-Skill detection requires both broader cross-source benchmark coverage and evaluation that jointly measures attack detection and benign over-flagging.

</details>


### [236] [Securing Filesystems for Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19924)
*Dimitra Giantsidi,Antoine Delignat-Lavaud,Cédric Fournet,Jinnan Guo,Heidi Howard,Tianjiao Huang,Kapil Vaswani,Stavros Volos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShieldFS是一个POSIX兼容的文件系统，在机密计算威胁模型下为持久存储提供端到端的完整性和新鲜性保证，无需修改应用程序。它通过将文件系统状态表示为简洁的密码学承诺并维护在TEE内，利用哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树认证磁盘数据结构，通过事务和写时复制原子更新状态与承诺。基于ZFS的实现ShieldZFS在标准基准测试和真实负载下性能与最先进文件系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算虽能保护TEE内部的应用，但存储仍然易受攻击。即使有磁盘加密，恶意的云提供商仍可对磁盘状态进行回滚、重放、分叉或篡改，破坏有状态应用所需的完整性和新鲜性保证。现有解决方案要么假设受信任的存储，要么产生高昂开销，要么将完整性逻辑推入应用程序。

Method: ShieldFS将允许的文件系统状态表示为简洁的密码学承诺，保存在TEE内部并复制到轻量级可信注册表中。盘上数据结构（包括预写日志和存储池）通过哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树进行认证。采用事务和写时复制机制原子更新持久文件系统状态和承诺，读操作时验证承诺，确保在整个I/O栈不受信任时仍能检测回滚、重放和歧义攻击。实现方式为扩展ZFS得到ShieldZFS。

Result: 标准文件系统基准测试和真实工作负载评估表明，ShieldZFS在提供强完整性和新鲜性保证的同时，性能与最先进的文件系统相当。

Conclusion: ShieldFS证明了在不修改应用程序的前提下，可以在完全不受信任的I/O栈环境中为持久存储提供端到端的完整性和新鲜性保证，且性能开销可接受，为机密计算场景下安全持久存储提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects applications inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but it leaves storage vulnerable. Even with disk encryption, a malicious cloud provider can roll back, replay, fork, or tamper with disk state, breaking the integrity and freshness guarantees required by stateful applications. Existing solutions either assume trusted storage, incur high overheads, or push integrity logic into applications. We present ShieldFS, a POSIX-compliant filesystem that provides end-to-end integrity and freshness for persistent storage in the confidential-computing threat model without requiring application changes. ShieldFS represents permissible filesystem states using succinct cryptographic commitments, maintained inside TEEs and replicated in a lightweight trusted registry. On-disk data structures, including a write-ahead log and a storage pool, are authenticated using hash chains and an embedded Merkle tree. ShieldFS utilizes transactions and copy-on-write to update persistent filesystem state and commitments atomically. The commitments are verified during reads, ensuring that rollback, replay, and equivocation attacks are detected even when the entire I/O stack is untrusted. We implement the design by extending ZFS, yielding ShieldZFS. Evaluation with standard filesystem benchmarks and real-world workloads shows that ShieldZFS provides strong integrity and freshness guarantees with performance comparable to state-of-the-art filesystems.

</details>


### [237] [ShadowPath: Lookup-Private Credential Status Verification over Authenticated State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19937)
*Patrick Herbke,Wolf Rieder,Christian René Sechting,Huaning Yang,Sid Lamichhane,Philip Raschke,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShadowPath提出了一种将可验证凭证撤销检查移至持有者端的方案，通过零知识证明来实现隐私保护的凭证状态查询，并首次对Verkle树和稀疏Merkle树在凭证撤销场景中的性能进行了评估比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证凭证的撤销检查在现有方案中可能泄露重复标识符、注册表位置或请求元数据，这些信息可作为稳定句柄将不同的展示会话关联起来，破坏隐私模型。需要一种在确保撤销检查有效的同时不泄露可关联元数据的方案。

Method: ShadowPath将凭证状态查询移至持有者端。每次展示时，持有者以零知识方式证明凭证在验证者选择的注册表根下未被撤销。验证者仅获知状态结果而不获取可观察元数据。本文使用Groth16证明系统，比较了Verkle树和稀疏Merkle树在凭证撤销场景中的应用效果，并使用会话随机性来保证每次展示的独立性问题。

Result: 在30次桌面试验中，使用稀疏Merkle树的中位Groth16证明时间为371.6ms，使用Verkle树为2.11s；验证时间分别为3.70ms和7.55ms。Verkle树的Groth16证明在两种主流移动设备上均约需3秒。结果表明更短的认证路径并不必然带来更便宜的零知识证明。

Conclusion: ShadowPath通过将撤销检查移至持有者端并利用零知识证明，有效防止了凭证状态数据被用作链接不同展示的稳定句柄。Verkle树虽能缩短认证路径，但其基于KZG的认证成本更高，导致证明时间反而长于稀疏Merkle树。在会话值独立性的假设下，验证者可见的状态数据不会泄露两次展示是否使用同一凭证，但该保证不包括发行者与验证者串通及同步流量场景。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials let holders present digitally signed claims without requiring the issuer to participate in every presentation. Revocation complicates this privacy model because a verifier must determine whether a credential remains valid. Existing status checks may expose recurring identifiers, registry positions, or request metadata. Such information can serve as stable handles to link separate presentations. ShadowPath moves the credential status lookup to the holder. For each presentation, the holder proves, in zero-knowledge, that the credential has not been revoked under the verifier-selected registry root. The verifier learns the status result but not observable metadata. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first evaluation of Verkle trees for credential revocation and compare them with sparse Merkle trees to assess their applicability in real world applications. The comparison tests whether reducing path depth with Verkle trees offsets the higher cost of KZG-based authentication. Across 30 desktop trials, median Groth16 proving took 371.6ms with sparse Merkle and 2.11s with Verkle. Verification took 3.70ms and 7.55ms, respectively. Groth16 Verkle proving took about 3s on both primary mobile devices. The results show that shorter authenticated paths do not necessarily yield cheaper zero-knowledge proofs. With fresh session randomness, verifier-visible status data do not reveal whether two presentations use the same credential under the stated assumption of session-value independence. This guarantee excludes issuer-verifier collusion and synchronization traffic.

</details>


### [238] [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938)
*Christopher Henshaw,Gour Karmakar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指令微调LLM的标准分类框架，用于检测异常认证行为（包括边界案例）。在受控网络靶场上生成端点特定的认证数据，评估了三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen 2.5 7B、GPT-OSS 20B）与传统方法（Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch统计异常检测）的对比。Meta Llama表现最佳（准确率89.3%，F1 91.8%），在边界案例检测上显著优于基线（80% vs 20%/15%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有日志异常检测方法存在局限：Wazuh依赖预定义规则难以应对未知威胁，OpenSearch等统计方法缺乏语义理解，而LLM方法受提示构造、日志噪声和通用数据集缺少端点特定认证行为的影响。作者旨在构建一个标准化框架，解决LLM在日志异常检测中的这些局限性，特别是边界案例检测问题。

Method: 首先开发受控网络安全测试平台生成端点特定认证数据，构建包含正常、边界和异常三类行为场景的精选数据集。其次设计标准化的基于指令的分类框架（instruction-based classification framework）。然后评估三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct和GPT-OSS 20B），并与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch异常检测在统一的地面真值严重性框架下进行比较分析。

Result: Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct取得最优端到端性能：准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1分数91.8%、假阴性率11.8%。对比之下，Wazuh准确率仅52.0%、假阴性率68.6%；OpenSearch准确率49.3%、假阴性率74.5%。Meta Llama检测出80%的边界异常场景，而Wazuh和OpenSearch分别仅检测出20%和15%。Qwen性能较低但推理延迟最低且结构化响应有效性达100%；GPT-OSS在有效响应时表现良好。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于指令微调的LLM框架在异常认证行为检测上显著优于传统规则和统计方法，尤其在边界案例检测方面具有明显优势。这验证了LLM利用语义和上下文信息的能力对日志异常检测的价值，同时指出不同LLM在性能、延迟和响应可靠性上存在权衡，需要根据实际部署需求进行选择。

Abstract: Existing approaches to anomalous behaviour log detection, such as Wazuh rely primarily on predefined detection rules, while statistical anomaly detection approaches such as OpenSearch identify deviations from previously observed behavioural patterns. Recent research has investigated LLMs for log anomaly detection because of their ability to interpret semantic and contextual information. However, LLM-based approaches can be affected by prompt construction, noisy log data, and reliance on generic datasets that may lack endpoint-specific authentication behaviours. To address these limitations, this study develops a standardised instruction-based LLM classification framework for detecting anomalous authentication behaviours, including borderline cases. A controlled cybersecurity testbed was developed to generate endpoint-specific authentication data, producing a curated dataset comprising normal, borderline, and anomalous behavioural scenarios. Three instruction-tuned LLMs, Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct, and GPT-OSS 20B, were evaluated against Wazuh rule-based detection and OpenSearch Anomaly Detection using a common ground-truth severity framework. Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct achieved the strongest overall end-to-end detection performance, with an accuracy of 89.3%, recall of 88.2%, F1-score of 91.8%, and false negative rate of 11.8%. In comparison, Wazuh achieved an accuracy of 52.0% and false negative rate of 68.6%, while OpenSearch achieved an accuracy of 49.3% and false negative rate of 74.5%. Meta Llama also detected 80% of the borderline anomalous scenarios, compared with 20% for Wazuh and 15% for OpenSearch. Qwen achieved lower overall detection performance than Meta Llama but recorded the lowest average inference latency and 100% structured-response validity. GPT-OSS demonstrated strong classification performance when valid responses were produced.

</details>


### [239] [Tracking the Trend in How Speech Synthesizers Deceive People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19959)
*Milan Šalko,Anton Firc,Kamil Malinka,Vojtěch Staněk,Martin Perešini,Filip Pleško,Jakub Reš*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了人类与预训练检测器对现代语音合成工具（2019/2022/2024年发布）生成深度伪造音频的检测能力。研究发现，全合成语音的F1分数从约90%骤降至ElevenLabs的48%，部分替换场景下严格准确率仅9%，且真假语音误判互补，人类与检测器均无法可靠定位短时篡改，表明仅靠人类感知不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期研究虽报告人类对深度伪造语音有70-80%的检测准确率，但这些研究主要基于较旧的语音合成器。随着语音合成技术快速进步，需要验证人类检测能力是否仍然有效，以及人类与自动检测器的相对表现。

Method: 选取2019（RTVC）、2022（YourTTS）和2024（ElevenLabs）三个时间点的语音合成工具，招募82名IT专业人员参与感知实验，同时用六个预训练检测器在同一材料上进行基准测试。实验涵盖全合成语音（full spoofs）和部分替换语音（partial spoofs）两种条件。

Result: 全合成语音条件下，F1分数从RTVC和YourTTS的约90%下降到ElevenLabs的48%；部分替换条件下严格准确率仅9%，且被试将合成句误判为真话（bona fide）的比例达77%。被试还越来越多地将真实语音误判为伪造。人类与检测器的失败模式互补，均无法可靠定位短篡改片段。

Conclusion: 人类感知对现代合成和部分伪造条件不可靠，研究结果支持采用程序性验证、来源认证、水印和片段级检测等机制来保障语音内容的真实性。

Abstract: Advances in speech synthesis have made deepfake audio highly realistic. Earlier studies reported 70-80% human detection accuracy, but relied primarily on older synthesizers. We compare human detection for three selected voice synthesis tools released in 2019, 2022, and 2024 with 82 IT professionals, and benchmark humans against six pretrained detectors on the same material. For fully synthetic speech (full spoofs), the F1 score drops from about 90% for RTVC and YourTTS to 48% for ElevenLabs, although listeners were explicitly warned that deepfakes were present. For partial spoofing, where only one sentence of an utterance is altered, strict accuracy falls to 9%, and listeners classify the synthetic sentence as bona fide 77% of the time. Humans and detectors fail in complementary ways, and neither reliably localizes short manipulations. Additionally, listeners increasingly mislabel bona fide speech as fake, eroding trust in unmanipulated audio. These findings show that human perception alone is unreliable for the selected modern and partial-spoof conditions and motivate procedural verification, provenance, watermarking, and segment-level detection.

</details>


### [240] [COPA: Continual Preference Optimization for Adaptive Prompt Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19982)
*Roshan Sood,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: COPA是一个持续偏好优化框架，将提示注入攻击防御视为终身学习问题，通过GRPO优化的不断增量和边际加权经验重放，实现对新出现的威胁持续适应，同时缓解灾难性遗忘并保持通用能力。与最先进防御相比，攻击成功率平均降低4.4倍（最大6.3倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM提示注入攻击防御大多是静态的，依赖固定对齐目标或攻击特定过滤机制，无法适应不断演化的对抗攻击。近期终身对齐方法虽能处理变化的用户偏好，但不考虑持续演化以利用已学防御弱点的自适应攻击者。在实际部署中，攻击分布不断演化，必须持续适应且不能牺牲对先前威胁的鲁棒性。

Method: COPA将提示注入防御构建为终身学习问题，采用两个核心机制：(1) 基于GRPO的强化学习优化，从新观察到的攻击中逐步引入反馈；(2) 边际加权经验重放（margin-weighted experience replay），用于保留对先前攻击类别的防御能力。整体框架持续将新攻击流纳入优化，同时防止灾难性遗忘并维持模型通用能力。

Result: 在终身提示注入攻击流的评测中，COPA相比最先进防御方法将攻击成功率最高降低6.3倍，平均降低4.4倍。

Conclusion: 持续偏好优化是防御LLM面对自适应对抗者的有效范式，COPA证明了在终身学习框架下，模型可以在不断适应新威胁的同时保持对旧攻击的鲁棒性和通用能力。

Abstract: LLMs remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, where adversarial instructions embedded in user inputs or external content manipulate model behavior and bypass safeguards. Existing defenses are predominantly static, relying on fixed alignment objectives or attack-specific filtering mechanisms that require redesign as new attack strategies emerge. While recent lifelong alignment methods address shifting user preferences, they do not account for adaptive adversaries that continually evolve to exploit weaknesses in previously learned defenses. This limitation is particularly important in real-world deployments, where evolving attack distributions necessitate continual adaptation without sacrificing robustness to previously encountered threats. We present COPA, a continual preference optimization framework that treats prompt-injection defense as a lifelong learning problem. Instead of one-time alignment, COPA incrementally incorporates feedback from newly observed attacks via GRPO-based optimization and uses margin-weighted experience replay to retain defenses against prior attack classes. This enables continuous adaptation to emerging threats while mitigating catastrophic forgetting and preserving general-purpose model capabilities. Across lifelong prompt injection attack streams, COPA reduces attack success rate by up to 6.3x and 4.4x on average compared to state-of-the-art defenses. These results highlight continual preference optimization as an effective paradigm for defending LLMs against adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [241] [EchoCoT: Extracting Hidden Chain-of-Thought from Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20055)
*Yiting Qu,Ziqing Yang,Chi Cui,Ye Leng,Junjie Chu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了能否通过API交互从黑盒大型推理模型（LRMs）中近逐字提取隐藏的思维链（CoT）轨迹。作者发现了工具调用之间被忽视的"推理回放"攻击面，并开发了EchoCoT——一种多步攻击方法，利用API返回的保真度信号迭代提取隐藏CoT。在开源LRM上实现了最高66.4%的近逐字提取成功率，在Gemini-2.5上成功提取了33,463个token的CoT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿专有大型推理模型（LRMs）的隐藏思维链（CoT）轨迹是极具价值的模型资产，但隐藏CoT能否从黑盒模型中直接提取的问题此前尚未被充分探索。本研究旨在填补这一空白，揭示隐藏CoT提取作为实际安全风险的可能性。

Method: 作者首先识别出一个被忽视的攻击面——工具调用之间的"推理回放"表面（reasoning replay surface）；然后开发EchoCoT，一种多步攻击方法，通过API返回的保真度信号迭代提取隐藏CoT；同时构建了基于LLM的优化框架，可自动搜索跨数据集有效的通用注入轨迹。

Result: 在三个开源和五个前沿专有LRM上进行评估。开源LRM上EchoCoT达到最高66.4%的近逐字提取成功率（提取轨迹长度与目标偏差≤10%，至少90%的token与目标CoT完全匹配）；同一注入轨迹可泛化到未见数据集，最高达80%提取成功率。专有LRM上提取的CoT与供应商报告的长度和可用摘要高度吻合；在Gemini-2.5上成功从32,948-token目标中提取出33,463个token。

Conclusion: 研究结果证实了隐藏CoT提取是一种实际的、可操作的安全风险，强调了需要更好地保护隐藏CoT资产的重要性。

Abstract: Hidden chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, especially those from frontier proprietary large reasoning models (LRMs), are valuable model assets. Yet whether these hidden CoTs can be directly extracted from black-box models remains largely unexplored. In this work, we systematically study whether hidden CoTs can be extracted near-verbatim from black-box LRMs through API interactions. We identify a previously overlooked reasoning replay surface between tool calls and develop EchoCoT, a multi-step attack that iteratively extracts hidden CoTs using API-returned fidelity signals. We further develop an LLM-based optimization framework that automatically searches for an effective universal injection trajectory across various datasets. We evaluate EchoCoT on three open-source and five frontier proprietary LRMs. On open-source LRMs, EchoCoT achieves up to 66.4\% near-verbatim extraction success, with the extracted trace length within 10\% of the target and at least 90\% of tokens exactly matching the target CoT. The same injection trajectory also generalizes to unseen datasets, achieving up to 80\% extraction success under the same criterion. For tested frontier proprietary LRMs, a substantial fraction of extracted CoTs closely align with provider-reported reasoning lengths and available CoT summaries. EchoCoT can also extract very long CoTs: on Gemini-2.5, it extracts 33,463 tokens from a 32,948-token target. These results establish hidden-CoT extraction as a practical security risk and highlight the need to better protect hidden CoT assets.

</details>


### [242] [A Meta-Study on Replication Papers in Usable Security & Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20108)
*Christian Mack,Benjamin Berens,Hanna Algedri,Tobias Hilt,Daniela Reimer,Peter Mayer,Melanie Volkamer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法（分析13个会议的征稿启事、系统搜索2016-2025年间24篇复制研究、运用Olszewski等人的复制分类法、并对作者进行调查）系统梳理了可用性与安全隐私（usable security and privacy）领域中复制研究的现状，揭示四个关键洞察：征稿指南需更清晰、复制论文的修改细节难以辨识、严格的精确复制不存在、时间与情境变化是最常见的复制动机，并据此提出针对会议、研究者和审稿人的实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用性与安全隐私研究领域仍处于发展阶段，缺乏成熟的研究标准（尤其是复制研究方面），因此需要系统了解该领域复制的当前状态，以推动研究规范的建立。

Method: 采用混合方法：(1) 检视13个安全、隐私和人机交互领域的会议征稿启事；(2) 在2016-2025年间系统搜索发表在相关会议上的复制用户研究论文，共获得24篇；(3) 运用Olszewski等人（2025）提出的复制分类法对这24篇论文进行分类；(4) 向这些论文作者发放调查问卷以了解其复制研究的动机。

Result: 分析得出四个关键发现：(A) 征稿启事需要为作者和审稿人提供更清晰的复制工作指南；(B) 阅读复制论文时难以确定相对原始研究做了哪些修改；(C) 样本中不存在严格的精确复制，约三分之二的研究修改了原始工作的多个方面；(D) 时间和情境变化影响研究结果是最常被引用的复制动机之一。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，作者为会议主办方、研究者和同行审稿人提供了加强复制实践的具体建议，以提升可用性与安全隐私研究领域的复制质量和标准。

Abstract: The field of usable security and privacy research is a young and expanding field, which is still developing standards for its research, e.g. regarding replications. We used a mixed-method approach, in order to get a better understanding of the current state of replications in the field of usable security and privacy: (1) we examine the Call for Papers of 13 venues spanning security, privacy, and human-computer interaction; (2) we conduct a systematic search for papers reporting replicated user studies published across these venues between 2016 and 2025, yielding 24 relevant publications; (3) we categorized these 24 papers employing the replication taxonomy proposed by Olszewski et al. (2025); (4) we distributed a survey to the authors of these papers to understand their motivations for conducting replications. Our analysis reveals four key insights: (A) Calls for Papers would benefit from clearer guidelines for authors and reviewers regarding replication work; (B) determining what modifications were made relative to the original study proves difficult when reading replication papers; (C) strict exact replications do not exist in our sample. Approximately two-thirds of the 24 studies altered multiple aspects of the original work; (D) temporal and contextual changes affecting results emerged as one of the most frequently cited motivations for replication. Based on these findings, we offer practical recommendations for venues, researchers, and peer reviewers to strengthen replication practices in usable security and privacy research.

</details>


### [243] [Privacy-Preserving Detection of Rare Disease-Associated Cell Subsets via Secure Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20118)
*Ş. Selcan Magara,Esther Havemann,Debora Jutz,Ali Burak Ünal,Mete Akgün*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，能够在秘密共享数据上训练和推理CellCnn卷积神经网络，用于检测罕见疾病相关细胞亚群，在保护患者数据隐私的同时保持接近明文模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测罕见疾病相关细胞亚群对理解白血病和病毒感染等疾病至关重要，但训练可靠的CellCnn模型需要跨机构的大规模患者队列。然而，单细胞数据过于敏感，在现有隐私法规下无法跨机构共享，因此需要一种能保护隐私的分布式训练方案。

Method: 提出一个基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，使CellCnn的完整训练和推理过程在秘密共享数据上执行。该框架确保参与方和计算服务器都无法观察到原始患者数据或中间值，同时保留了之前隐私保护方法中被移除的ReLU激活函数和偏置项等关键架构组件。

Result: 在巨细胞病毒感染（CMV）和急性髓系白血病（AML）的基准单细胞数据集上评估，该实现保持接近明文对比版本的准确率，同时优于先前的隐私保护基线方法。

Conclusion: 该MPC框架成功地在不暴露原始患者数据的前提下实现了CellCnn的准确训练与推理，保留了完整架构组件，为跨机构协作的罕见疾病细胞亚群检测提供了一种可行的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: The detection of rare disease-associated cell subsets from high-dimensional single-cell measurements is critical for understanding diseases such as leukaemia and viral infections. CellCnn, a convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for this task, has demonstrated the ability to identify phenotype-associated cell populations at frequencies as low as 0.01\%. Training such models reliably requires patient cohorts that are larger and more diverse than any single institution can typically assemble, and the underlying single-cell data is too sensitive to share across institutional boundaries under existing privacy regulations. We propose a secure multi-party computation (MPC) framework that enables the training and inference of CellCnn entirely on secret-shared data. This ensures that neither the participants nor the computing servers ever observe raw patient data or intermediate values. Evaluated on benchmark single-cell datasets for cytomegalovirus infection (CMV) and acute myeloid leukaemia (AML), our implementation preserves accuracy close to its plaintext counterpart while outperforming the prior privacy-preserving baseline. In contrast to earlier privacy-preserving approaches that removed components such as ReLU activations and bias terms, our method retains these key parts of the CellCnn architecture and supports accurate analysis without exposing raw patient data.

</details>


### [244] [Trustworthy mobile edge caching: a blockchain approach to mitigate malicious nodes and incentivize cache sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20145)
*Motahare Ebrahimi,Nastooh Taheri Javan,Seyedakbar Mostafavi,Fatemeh Pakzaban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区块链的信任管理机制，用于解决移动边缘网络中恶意边缘服务器和自私助手移动节点的缓存共享问题，通过智能合约计算直接和间接信任、引入奖励机制激励缓存共享，并利用区块链认证防止节点冒充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络流量持续增长，边缘服务器内容缓存对降低延迟至关重要，但面临两个关键挑战：一是恶意边缘服务器可能删除或篡改缓存内容，二是边缘服务器容量有限。为克服容量限制，引入助手移动节点贡献缓存资源，但这些节点存在自私行为和恶意风险，因此需要设计有效的信任管理和激励机制。

Method: 该机制包括以下核心组件：(1)基于区块链的信任管理，通过智能合约计算直接和间接信任值，准确过滤恶意节点；(2)信任度基于移动节点对服务质量的满意度来确定，信任数据安全存储在区块链上；(3)引入奖励机制激励节点共享缓存资源；(4)基于区块链的认证机制防止节点身份冒充；(5)优化目标综合考虑信任、缓存容量和成本效率，并考虑移动节点移动性、能耗和共识过程中的计算能力约束。

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使在数据中存在10%噪声的情况下，所提出的方法也能准确区分诚实和恶意的服务器。

Conclusion: 该基于区块链的信任管理机制能够有效解决移动边缘网络中恶意节点和信任问题，在存在数据噪声的情况下仍能保持较高的区分准确性，为边缘缓存安全提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: As mobile network traffic continues to grow, content caching on edge servers is critical for reducing latency. However, challenges such as malicious edge servers that may delete or manipulate cached content, along with the limited capacity of these servers, need to be addressed. To overcome the capacity limitations, helper mobile nodes can contribute their cache resources. However, due to their selfish behavior, an incentive mechanism is necessary to encourage resource sharing. Additionally, these helper nodes can also be malicious. This paper proposes a blockchain-based trust management mechanism that addresses these challenges by accurately identifying trustworthy edge servers and mobile nodes. The proposed mechanism calculates both direct and indirect trust using smart contracts, ensuring that malicious nodes are effectively filtered out. Trustworthiness is determined based on mobile node satisfaction with the quality of service, and trust data is securely stored on the blockchain. To combat node selfishness, a reward mechanism is introduced to incentivize cache sharing. Furthermore, a blockchain-based authentication mechanism protects against node impersonation. Our approach optimizes trust, cache capacity, and cost efficiency while considering mobile node mobility, energy consumption, and computational power constraints during the consensus process. Simulation results show that the proposed method can accurately distinguish between honest and malicious servers, even with a 10% noise in data.

</details>


### [245] [Chameleon: Robust Defense Against Tor Website Fingerprinting via Many-to-Many Traffic Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20160)
*Yuwen Cui,Kai Wei,Kehan Shen,Ning Wang,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Chameleon，一种基于多对多随机流量变形的Tor网站指纹（WF）防御方法。它通过选择高类内多样性、低类间差异的变形候选，将网页轨迹随机映射到多个候选目标，增加攻击者不确定性。在三个公开数据集上的闭集和开集设置下，Chameleon在对抗训练攻击准确率、带宽和时间开销方面均显著优于现有防御方法，并在真实PT桥接部署中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WF防御大多产生可学习的网络轨迹映射特征，且对抗训练鲁棒性不等同于对防御感知自编码器（DAAE）攻击的鲁棒性。现有防御在面对更强大的攻击时存在根本性不足，需要一种更具鲁棒性的防御方案。

Method: Chameleon采用多对多随机流量变形策略：选择高类内多样性和低类间差异的变形候选，将每个网页轨迹随机映射到多个候选，并允许不同网页共享变形目标以增加对抗不确定性。为实用Tor部署，引入基于基数树（radix-trie）的同步机制，使可插拔传输（PT）端点通过包方向前缀识别一致的变形轨迹，并结合轨迹变异和归一化前缀匹配降低开销。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上对抗六种SOTA防御和五种WF攻击的评估中，相比Adaptive Tamaraw，Chameleon将对抗训练攻击准确率最多降低36.74%，同时带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%。在GTT23上的DAAE-RF攻击下，Chameleon将攻击F1-score限制在35.19%，而Adaptive Tamaraw只能限制到88.22%。真实PT桥接评估中仅引入16.25%时间开销。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过多对多随机流量变形和高效的PT同步机制，在显著降低强WF攻击效果的同时保持了较低的开销，为Tor提供了实用且鲁棒的WF防御方案。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer users' browsing activities from encrypted Tor traffic by exploiting side-channel features. Although many WF defenses have been proposed, we find that most existing defenses create learnable web trace mapping features. We further show that robustness against adversarial training does not necessarily imply robustness against defense-aware autoencoder (DAAE)-based attacks.
  To address these limitations, we present Chameleon, a robust WF defense based on many-to-many randomized traffic morphing. Chameleon selects morphing candidates with high intra-class diversity and low inter-class disparity. Chameleon randomly maps each webpage trace to multiple candidates, and allows different webpages to share morphing targets, thereby increasing adversarial uncertainty. For practical Tor deployment, Chameleon introduces a radix-trie-based synchronization mechanism that enables pluggable transport (PT) endpoints to identify consistent morphing traces using packet-direction prefixes, together with trace mutation and normalized prefix matching to reduce overhead. We evaluate Chameleon against six state-of-the-art defenses and five WF attacks on three public datasets in closed- and open-world settings. Compared with Adaptive Tamaraw, Chameleon reduces adversarial-training-based attack accuracy by up to 36.74% while reducing bandwidth and time overhead by 34.12% and 60.38%, respectively. Under DAAE-based RF attacks on GTT23, Chameleon limits attack performance to 35.19% F1-score while Adaptive Tamaraw only limits it to 88.22% F1-score. In the real-world PT bridge evaluation, Chameleon substantially reduces the effectiveness of strong WF attacks while incurring only 16.25% time overhead.

</details>


### [246] [VQC-ZTI: Variational Quantum Control for Zero Trust Protection of the Tactile Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18572)
*Mubassir Serneabat Sudipto,Shakil Ahmed,Ashfaq Khokhar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VQC-ZTI框架，一种分裂平面变分量子分类器框架，用于触觉互联网服务的零信任保护。该框架将变异量子分类器（VQC）的异常评分与策略执行解耦，在离线路径分析加密流遥测数据，在线策略引擎应用确定性动作。在CESNET数据集上，混合量子神经网络在随机、实体分组和时间划分场景下分别达到0.9981、0.9974和0.9941的平均AUC，并相比ExtraTrees将假阳性率降低44.6%至67.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉互联网服务将网络事件与物理驱动直接耦合，安全决策需要在不干扰控制路径的前提下提升风险区分能力。现有方案可能因安全检测扰动控制回路而影响实时性，因此需要一种既能增强安全检测又不影响控制路径的零信任保护方案。

Method: 提出VQC-ZTI分裂平面框架：离线路径中的变分量子分类器分析加密流遥测数据进行异常评分，在线策略引擎应用缓存的确定性授权、限制、升级和拒绝动作。通过解耦异常评分与策略执行，实现检测灵敏度与策略激进程度的独立调优。实现采用混合PyTorch-PennyLane架构，在CESNET衍生聚合流量上用随机、实体分组和时间三种划分方式评估全混合量子神经网络。

Result: 全混合量子神经网络在三种评估场景下平均AUC分别为0.9981、0.9974和0.9941，相比ExtraTrees将假阳性率分别降低44.6%、49.6%和67.9%。组件时序分解表明批处理VQC评分处于异步证据路径而非即时执行路径。

Conclusion: VQC-ZTI通过分裂平面设计有效实现了触觉互联网服务的安全增强与控制路径保持，量子分类器的检测性能显著优于经典基线，且解耦架构保证了控制行为的可预测性。该框架在保障实时控制的同时提供了可独立调优的安全机制。

Abstract: Tactile Internet services couple cyber events directly to physical actuation, so security decisions must improve risk discrimination without perturbing the control path. This paper presents VQC-ZTI, a split-plane Variational Quantum Classifier framework for zero-trust protection of Tactile Internet services, in which an off-path VQC analyzes encrypted-flow telemetry while an on-path policy engine applies cached deterministic grant, restrict, step-up, and deny actions. By decoupling anomaly scoring from enforcement, VQC-ZTI preserves predictable control behavior and allows detector sensitivity and policy aggressiveness to be tuned independently. We evaluate the framework on CESNET-derived aggregated traffic using random, entity-group, and temporal holdouts with a hybrid PyTorch-PennyLane implementation. The full-hybrid Quantum Neural Network achieves mean areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.9981, 0.9974, and 0.9941 and reduces the false-positive rate relative to ExtraTrees by 44.6%, 49.6%, and 67.9%, respectively. A representative component-timing decomposition further illustrates that batched VQC scoring remains in the asynchronous evidence path rather than the immediate enforcement path.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [PEtab SciML: an exchange format for specifying and training dynamic scientific machine learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20184)
*Sebastian Persson,Branwen Snelling,Maren Philipps,Daniel Weindl,Marija Cvijovic,Jan Hasenauer,Dilan Pathirana,Fabian Fröhlich*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: PEtab SciML是一个用于指定参数估计问题的互操作数据格式，支持在科学机器学习（SciML）中联合估计机理常微分方程（ODE）和机器学习（ML）组件参数，并提供了参考Python库、Python/JAX和Julia的建模支持以及真实数据基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态科学机器学习（SciML）模型结合了机理ODE与ML组件，在未知生物过程学习和辅助数据模态整合中有广泛应用。然而，要实现可复现且高效的SciML训练，需要一种能统一指定参数估计问题的互操作数据格式，这正是该工作要解决的问题。

Method: 本文提出PEtab SciML，一种用于指定参数估计问题的互操作数据格式，支持在现实问题设置中多种ML-ODE混合模式，并配套提供参考Python库、基于AMICI和PEtab.jl的Python/JAX与Julia下游建模支持，以及一组真实数据基准。

Result: PEtab SciML已在GitHub上发布，参考Python包可通过PyPI安装，并在Linux、macOS和Windows上持续测试和支持。

Conclusion: PEtab SciML为实现可复现、高效的SciML训练提供了标准化基础设施，通过统一的数据格式和跨生态工具支持，促进了机理模型与机器学习组件联合参数估计的实践。

Abstract: Summary: Dynamic scientific machine learning (SciML) models that combine mechanistic ordinary differential equations (ODEs) with machine learning (ML) components have applications ranging from learning unknown biological processes to integrating auxiliary data modalities into dynamic modelling. To enable reproducible and efficient SciML training, we introduce PEtab SciML, an interoperable data format for specifying parameter estimation problems in which mechanistic and ML model parameters are jointly estimated from time series data. PEtab SciML supports several ML ODE hybridization patterns in realistic problem setups. It is accompanied by a reference Python library and downstream modelling support in Python/JAX and Julia, provided by AMICI and PEtab.jl, respectively, and a collection of real data benchmarks. Availability and implementation: PEtab SciML is available on GitHub (https://github.com/PEtab-dev/petab_sciml). The reference Python package is installable from PyPI and is continuously tested and supported on Linux, macOS, and Windows.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI
